Despite its compact design, it maintains competitive and, on several benchmarks, superior recognition accuracy.

</details>


### [34] [CoRe-SAM3: Conditional Semantic--Visual Reconciliation for SAM3 Crack Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05816)
*Shipeng Liu,Liang Zhao,Dengfeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对裂缝分割中模型难以准确识别细长、低对比度且拓扑连续的局部结构问题，分析了SAM3在裂缝数据集上的表现缺陷，发现其语义表示已包含大部分任务信息，而视觉表示的效用依赖于语义状态。为此提出条件语义-视觉调和方法CoRe，通过轻量级语义校准和空间对齐的视觉证据生成有界、正则化的残差，仅引入18.914K可训练参数，显著提升平均裂缝IoU（62.34%→70.47%）和clDice（81.98%→89.24%），同时有效纠正34.38%的原始错误，误伤率仅0.23%，验证了基于内部表示功能差异的约束性修正策略在视觉基础模型目标域适应中的有效性与高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAM3虽具强开放概念分割能力，但在裂缝分割任务中仍存在漏检弱裂缝、误激活背景区域及边界误差等问题，亟需一种高效、精准的领域适配策略以提升模型对细微、低对比度裂缝结构的捕捉能力。

Method: 提出条件语义-视觉调和（CoRe）方法：保留语义预测为主决策路径，采用轻量级语义校准调整目标域决策映射，并利用空间对齐的原生视觉证据生成零初始化、有界且正则化的条件残差，选择性修正已有预测结果。

Result: 在五个裂缝数据集上，CoRe-SAM3将平均裂缝IoU从62.34%提升至70.47%，clDice从81.98%提升至89.24%，仅增加18.914K可训练参数；预测转移分析显示，平均纠正34.38%的原始错误，对正确分类像素的误伤率仅为0.23%。

Conclusion: 基于内部表示功能差异的约束性预测修正策略，为具有强任务特定语义先验的视觉基础模型提供了有效且参数高效的跨域适应新范式，尤其适用于对细节敏感的医学或工业缺陷检测任务。

Abstract: Crack segmentation requires a model to recognize target semantics while accurately recovering thin, low-contrast, and topologically continuous local structures. Although SAM3 provides strong open-concept segmentation, its direct application to the crack domain still misses weak cracks, activates crack-like background regions, and produces local boundary errors. We first diagnose the functional differences between the internal prompt-conditioned semantic representation and native visual representation of SAM3 on five crack datasets. The results show that the semantic representation already carries most task information for crack prediction, whereas the utility of the visual representation depends on the current semantic state. Directly combining the two representations does not yield consistent gains. Based on this finding, we propose Conditional Semantic--Visual Reconciliation, termed CoRe. CoRe retains semantic prediction as the primary decision path, applies lightweight semantic calibration to adjust the target-domain decision mapping, and uses spatially aligned native visual evidence to generate a zero-initialized, bounded, and regularized conditional residual that selectively corrects existing predictions. Across five domains, CoRe-SAM3 improves the average Crack IoU from 62.34% to 70.47% and clDice from 81.98% to 89.24%, while introducing only 18.914 K trainable parameters. Prediction-transition analysis further shows that CoRe corrects an average of 34.38% of native errors, with a damage rate of only 0.23% on pixels correctly classified by native SAM3. These results demonstrate that constrained prediction correction based on the functional differences between internal representations provides an effective and parameter-efficient target-domain adaptation strategy for vision foundation models with strong task-specific semantic priors.

</details>


### [35] [Map the Possibilities: Spatial Belief Fields for Language-Goal Aerial Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05841)
*Haotian Xu,Yue Hu,Zhengqiu Zhu,Yong Zhao,Wenguan Wang,Quanjun Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SBFNav框架，通过语言条件下的空间信念场（SBF）建模多空间假设，解决语言引导飞行导航中因部分观测和模糊关系导致的空间不确定性问题。该方法在城市导航基准测试中显著提升成功率（SR）和路径长度归一化得分（SPL），尤其在未见测试集上表现优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将语言定位简化为单一航点或动作，过早地消除了不完整证据和模糊关系带来的空间不确定性，限制了导航性能。

Method: 引入基于语言条件的空间信念场（SBF），动态更新目标位置的可能分布，结合累积观测与指令选择最优度量航点进行控制，构建闭环导航框架。

Result: 在原始和修订版CityNav基准上均达到最佳性能；在Test Unseen子集上，SR从25.91%提升至32.29%，SPL从19.63%提升至30.43%。消融实验验证了空间信念建模优于单点预测。

Conclusion: SBFNav通过保留多个空间假设并动态更新信念分布，有效应对部分观测下的语言导航挑战，显著提升复杂环境中的导航准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Language-goal aerial navigation requires an agent to local- ize a potentially unobserved target from relational instruc- tions and partial observations, and translate this inference into metric actions in large-scale continuous environments. Existing methods often reduce language grounding to one single waypoint or action, prematurely collapsing the spatial uncertainty inherent in incomplete evidence and ambiguous relations. To address this limitation, we introduce SBFNav, a closed-loop navigation framework centered on a language- conditioned Spatial Belief Field (SBF). Unlike ego-centric maps that primarily record what has been observed, SBF rep- resents a task-conditioned distribution over plausible target locations, preserving multiple spatial hypotheses under par- tial evidence. At each step, this distribution is updated from accumulated observations as new evidence becomes avail- able. Built on this representation, SBFNav selects the goal that best aligns with the instruction and observations as a met- ric waypoint for control. Experiments on both the original and revised CityNav benchmarks achieve the best reported overall performance. On the Test Unseen split, our method improves SR from 25.91% to 32.29% and SPL from 19.63% to 30.43%. Ablation studies further confirm the advantages of spatial-belief modeling over single-point prediction.

</details>


### [36] [Hierarchical Prompt Injector for Domain Generalization Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05864)
*Xin Kun Lin,Ruoyu Guo,Jiaqi Guo,Maurice Pagnucco,Yang Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出空间分层提示（SHP）和分层提示注入器（HPI），通过区域级几何锚点增强类别提示，并实现空间自适应提示注入，提升域泛化语义分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法使用单一整体提示传递结构先验，且对所有像素统一应用提示，无法适应视角变化、遮挡等导致的部分可见情况，因此需要更精细的结构先验建模与空间自适应机制。

Method: 引入空间分层提示（SHP）以捕捉不同视角下的结构外观；设计分层提示注入器（HPI）基于视觉特征建模提示的语义相关性和空间影响，实现空间自适应注入；并引入辅助监督对齐提示与对应物体区域。

Result: 在合成到真实和真实到真实基准上分别达到70.62%和72.74%的mIoU，显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效提升了域泛化语义分割中结构先验的利用能力，通过空间自适应提示注入增强了模型对复杂场景变化的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Domain Generalized Semantic Segmentation (DGSS) is a challenging task, as vision models often rely on low-level appearance cues that change across domains. In contrast, structural attributes exhibit cross-domain stability, motivating the use of structural priors for DGSS. Existing methods use prompt learning to transfer such priors into DGSS models, but typically encode each class as a single holistic prompt. Moreover, these methods apply prompts uniformly to all pixels, offering no mechanism to adapt when only a subset of object regions is visible due to viewpoint changes, occlusion, and environmental variation. We address this with \textbf{Spatial Hierarchical Prompts (SHP)} that enrich each class with region-level geometric anchors capturing structural appearance from distinct viewing angles, ensuring complementary coverage under arbitrary viewpoints. Additionally, we propose the \textbf{Hierarchical Prompt Injector (HPI)}, which enables spatially adaptive prompt injection in foundation models. HPI spatially grounds prompts by modeling their semantic relevance and spatial influence with visual features. Considering the difficulty of learning spatially and semantically aware prompt injection, we further introduce auxiliary supervision to align hierarchical prompts with their corresponding object regions. We achieve 70.62\% and 72.74\% mIoU on synthetic-to-real and real-to-real benchmarks, respectively. Code and checkpoints are released at https://github.com/MosukFate/HPI

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

### [37] [When Agent Governance Helps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05531)
*Michael Ray Johnson,Linda Naimi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了受控自驱多智能体产品组织（GAMPO）框架，用于设计和评估在约束条件下追求自我生成目标的智能体系统。通过整合321份文献的定性证据，构建可运行规范，并在CHI-Bench医疗长程基准上测试其在开放模型与前沿模型中的表现。结果显示，治理效益取决于模型的剩余能力，且具有领域和模型特异性：在资源受限的开放模型中，完整流程无显著收益；而仅添加一句“验证你的写入”可使任务成功率从2/20提升至4/20。在前沿模型中，相同结构将先期授权成功率从24%提升至40%，但另一模型无增益，归因于其稳定拒绝建议的倾向。进一步优化为基于具体案例的、不依赖隐藏关键信息的“完成定义”，结合最佳五选一自一致性方法，使先期授权达84%（单次尝试68%），利用率管理达44%，但护理管理受内容质量限制。结论是：治理应根据剩余能力动态调整，且在前沿模型中，以案例为基础的规范优于统一流程。研究为探索性，样本量小（每单元5-25），仅一次试验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏对受控自驱型AI智能体组织的设计与评估标准，尤其在智能体自主设定目标但需在规则框架内运行的场景下。亟需一个可操作、可审计的框架来指导此类系统的构建与评估。

Method: 采用文档驱动的定性证据综合方法，分析321篇文献，整合代理理论、敏捷开发、平台架构与治理理论，构建GAMPO框架；随后在CHI-Bench医疗长周期基准上，对开放模型与前沿模型进行提示层实例化测试，评估不同治理策略的效果，并通过对比分析识别边界条件与关键影响因素。

Result: 治理效益高度依赖模型的剩余计算能力，且因模型和领域而异。在容量受限的开放模型中，完整治理流程无效；而简短提示‘验证你的写入’可提升成功率。在前沿模型中，同一结构使先期授权成功率从24%升至40%，但在另一模型中无效，源于其固有的推荐拒绝倾向。进一步引入基于案例的、答案无关的‘完成定义’机制，结合自一致性推理，使先期授权达84%（单次68%），利用率管理达44%，护理管理则受限于内容质量。

Conclusion: 本研究提出名为GAMPO的可审计框架，证明治理能力应根据模型剩余容量动态配置。在前沿模型中，基于具体案例定义的治理机制优于通用流程。研究结果具有探索性，样本有限，尚待大规模验证。

Abstract: No specification says how a governed autotelic AI agent organization, where agents pursue self-generated goals inside guardrails, should be designed and evaluated. We answer in two parts. First, we synthesize the Governed Autotelic Multi-Agent Product Organization (GAMPO) framework from a document-based qualitative evidence synthesis of 321 sources, integrating agency, agile, platform, and governance theory into a runnable specification. Second, we probe a prompt-layer instantiation of GAMPO on CHI-Bench, a long-horizon healthcare benchmark, across open and frontier models. The result is a boundary condition: governance benefit is gated by a model's spare capacity and is domain- and model-specific. On capacity-constrained open models the full procedure yields no reliable benefit, whereas a single "verify your writes" sentence doubles task success (pass@1 2/20 to 4/20). At the frontier the same scaffold lifts prior-authorization 24% to 40% but nets zero on another model, a gap traced to a stable recommendation-override disposition. A second result refines the first: replacing the generic procedure with an answer-blind, per-task definition-of-done, keyed only to the case's own policy and published standards, never the hidden key, raises prior-authorization to 84% under best-of-five self-consistency (68% single-attempt, confirmed by a held-out board) and utilization-management to 44%, while care-management meets a subjective content-quality wall. The contribution is a named, auditable framework and capability-gated evidence that governance should be sized to spare capacity, and that at the frontier a case-grounded specification beats a uniform procedure. Findings are exploratory: partial instantiation, small per-cell samples (n = 5-25), and single trials.

</details>


### [38] [TamilEOT: A Dataset and Model for Semantic End-of-Turn Detection in Tamil Telephone Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05631)
*Santhoshkumar V*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出并发布 TamilEOT 数据集，包含 18,485 个标注的泰米尔语对话回合边界，用于改进语音代理中的端到端说话人轮次检测。基于 Smart Turn v3 构建两个音频模型，在未见过的测试集上准确率从零样本的 70.30% 提升至 83.71%（8.7 MB）和 86.13%（21 MB），ROC-AUC 从 0.751 提升至 0.921。模型在笔记本 CPU 上单线程运行时间低于 150 ms。分析显示，只有编码器容量影响性能，其他因素如训练配置差异仅带来 0.87 点准确率波动。使用音频大模型标注可实现 97.5% 人类一致性，成本为 5.69 美元。此外，生产级语音活动检测与流式适配器引入额外 2.60 点性能损失，且 7.8% 的边界未被模型捕获。所有数据、权重、代码及负面结果均公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端说话人轮次检测模型多集中于主流语言，缺乏对南印度语言如泰米尔语的支持。当前依赖固定静音超时机制导致误中断或延迟响应，亟需基于语言特性的自动检测方法。

Method: 构建了包含真实电话对话的 TamilEOT 数据集；采用规则标签与音频大模型结合的方式进行标注；训练并评估基于 Smart Turn v3 的两个音频仅模型，分析不同训练参数对性能的影响，并量化生产流程中各组件带来的性能损耗。

Result: 模型在未见数据上达到最高 86.13% 准确率和 0.921 ROC-AUC；仅编码器容量显著影响性能；音频大模型标注成本低且一致性高；生产流程引入额外性能损失，约 7.8% 边界无法被模型感知。

Conclusion: TamilEOT 数据集与轻量级模型有效提升了泰米尔语语音交互中的端到端轮次检测性能，且整个系统设计透明可复现，为低资源语言语音交互提供重要基准。

Abstract: A voice agent has to decide, at every pause, whether the user has finished speaking. Without a model of the language that decision falls back to a fixed silence timeout: set it short and the agent interrupts, set it long and every turn pays the full wait. Open semantic end-of-turn detectors exist, but to our knowledge none covers a South Indian language. We release TamilEOT: 18,485 labelled turn boundaries cut from 116 real Tamil telephone conversations, and two audio-only detectors fine-tuned from Smart Turn v3. On a held-out split of 4,168 clips from 30 unseen calls, accuracy rises from 70.30% zero-shot to 83.71% (8.7 MB) and 86.13% (21 MB); ROC-AUC rises from 0.751 to 0.921. Both models run in under 150 ms single-threaded on a laptop CPU. We also report what building it cost. Rule-derived labels, checked against a blind human listening pass, were right 95.9% of the time on the positive class and 44.4% on the negative class, which is below chance, because the rule answered a different question than the model is asked. Replacing them with an audio-LLM labeller measured at 97.5% human agreement cost US$5.69. Of every training lever we measured, only encoder capacity moved the result; three runs at identical config and seed span 0.87 accuracy points, which is the floor below which none of our other deltas mean anything. Replaying the same labelled boundaries through the production VAD and streaming adapter costs a further 2.60 points, and 7.8% of boundaries are never surfaced to the model at all. Data, weights, code and every negative result are public.

</details>


### [39] [Better Together: Complementary Query Rewriting Under a Strong RAG Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05637)
*Sara Shanian,Xiaoqin Yi,Pavlo Ruban,Kurt MacDonald*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Query rewriting in RAG provides limited gains on its own but excels when combined with other methods due to complementary strengths. A confidence-gated router that applies rewriting only when needed captures ~50% of the benefit at <40% cost, improving answer quality without excessive overhead.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To evaluate whether query rewriting truly enhances RAG performance when the underlying search pipeline is already strong, and to explore cost-effective ways to leverage multiple rewriting strategies.

Method: Four query-rewriting strategies (S1-S4) are tested against two strong LLM baselines (HyDE, Query2Doc) across three datasets (HotpotQA, AmbigNQ, EnterpriseRAG-Bench) using paired-bootstrap significance tests. A confidence-gated router is simulated to apply rewriting only when baseline confidence is low.

Result: Combining methods yields significant gains: +12.5 points on enterprise data (51.70 vs 39.22), +1.6–1.8 on HotpotQA, but harms AmbigNQ (-2.4). Budget-matched controls explain only ~40% of the gain, confirming complementarity. The router achieves +4.3 HIT@10 at <40% cost and improves F1 by +1.92 (p<0.01).

Conclusion: Query rewriting should be treated as a complementary coverage mechanism, not a standalone replacement. Effective use requires cost-aware routing to balance performance and efficiency.

Abstract: A popular way to improve Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is to rewrite the user's question into several variants and search with all of them. We test whether this actually helps once the underlying search is already strong. Under one fixed, competitive pipeline (BGE dense retrieval, cross-encoder reranking, and MMR diversification), we compare four query-rewriting strategies (S1-S4) against two strong LLM baselines (HyDE, Query2Doc) on three datasets (HotpotQA, AmbigNQ, and the 512K-document EnterpriseRAG-Bench) over three seeds with paired-bootstrap significance tests. Our headline result is that rewriting alone is at best competitive with a strong baseline, but combining methods yields outsized gains because different strategies fail on different questions. A post-hoc union of four methods (S1+S3+S4+HyDE) improves HIT@10 over the baseline by +12.5 points on enterprise data (51.70 vs 39.22), and a five-method union reaches 52.98 (+13.8). Budget-matched controls capture only ~40% of this gain, confirming that complementarity, not retrieval budget, is the primary driver. On HotpotQA the union adds +1.6 to +1.8 points (p<0.001), saturating the all-method oracle; on AmbigNQ the same fusion hurts (-2.4 below the best solo, p<0.001), and we analyze when and why. Because rewriting is expensive, we evaluate in simulation a confidence-gated router that runs rewriting only when the baseline's own top-1 score is low. It captures about half of the enterprise full-merge gain (+4.3 HIT@10) while paying rewriting cost on <40% of queries, and automatically declines to rewrite on AmbigNQ. A downstream answer-quality evaluation confirms the router improves F1 by +1.92 (p<0.01) at roughly 40% of the expansion cost. In short: treat query rewriting as a complementary coverage source, applied through cost-aware routing, not as a standalone replacement for a strong baseline.

</details>


### [40] [Who Maintains Agent Skills? A Longitudinal Study of Human-Governed, AI-Assisted Skill Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05677)
*Chen Shen,Estevam Hruschka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析五个公开AI技能仓库的完整提交历史，揭示了终身大模型代理在实际部署中依赖外部技能文件（如SKILL.md）进行能力复用与维护的真实情况。研究发现：所有实质性编辑均由人类作者或合并贡献者完成，62%的编辑附带AI合著者标记，且编辑行为主要为内容增补与修正；然而，规则性（rule-likeness）的编码可靠性不足，表明当前尚无法可靠测量技能的规则化程度。研究释放了数据集、代码本和重放协议，强调未来自动化技能维护需建立在对人类治理与AI辅助协同机制的深刻理解之上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽尝试自动化技能维护，但忽视了人类维护作为关键瓶颈的作用，缺乏对真实维护流程的实证分析。本文旨在填补这一空白，直接考察公共技能仓库中技能编辑的实际过程。

Method: 通过挖掘五个代表性AI工具组织的公开仓库，收集2025年10月至2026年6月间的873次提交、143个技能文件及254次实质性编辑，采用预注册的治理、操作与触发证据代码本对每项编辑进行编码分析，并通过审计样本验证结果可靠性。

Result: 1. 所有实质性编辑均来自人类账户，62%包含AI合著者痕迹，不同仓库间差异显著；2. 编辑行为具有真实维护性质，以新增和修正为主；3. 预注册的规则性轴线未能通过信度检验，表明规则性难以从提交记录中可靠识别。

Conclusion: 当前公共技能维护并非完全自动化的流水线，而是一个由人类主导、AI辅助的迭代循环。未来的自动化技能维护系统必须基于对这一现实机制的理解来设计与评估。

Abstract: Lifelong LLM agents increasingly rely on external skill artifacts as one element for preserving and reusing capabilities over time. These skills (usually portable Markdown files such as SKILL.md) describe when and how to apply a capability and must be corrected, expanded, and consolidated as tools and usage patterns shift over deployment. Recent work seeks to automate skill curation, but it largely evaluates against automated baselines and treats human maintenance as an unmeasured bottleneck. We study that missing process directly. We mine the full commit histories of five public AI-skill repositories, a purposive sample of AI-tooling organizations, covering 873 commits, 143 skill files, and 254 substantive post-creation edits from October 2025 to June 2026. We code each edit with pre-registered governance, operation, and trigger-evidence codebooks. Three findings emerge. First, every substantive edit is authored or merged through a named human account, while 62% carry an AI co-author trailer, with large repository-level variation. Second, these edits are genuine curation: an audited sample shows that most change skill content, and the coded operations are dominated by additions and corrections. Third, a pre-registered rule-likeness axis fails its reliability gate; reliably coding rule-likeness from commit artifacts remains an open measurement problem. We release the corpus, codebooks, mining scripts, and a replay protocol for automated skill curators. For self-evolving agents, public skill maintenance currently looks less like an autonomous pipeline than a human-governed, AI-assisted loop that future curators must measure against and operate within.

</details>


### [41] [A Rubric-Guided Large Language Model Solution for Opioid Use Disorder Computable Phenotyping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05682)
*Mengxian Lyu,Paredes Pardo,Cheng Peng,Ziyi Chen,Mengyuan Zhang,Jieting Li Lu,Gary M Reisfield,William M Greene,Jenny Lo-Ciganic,Yonghui Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种基于专家制定的18项检查清单指导的大语言模型（LLM）框架，结合优化提示（OPRO）技术，用于准确识别电子健康记录（EHR）中的阿片类药物使用障碍（OUD）。该方法通过自动提取临床文本中的关键证据来判断OUD标志，经两位医生对253名患者的病历进行人工审查（其中68例为阳性），结果显示该模型在F1分数上达到0.774，AUROC为0.934，优于传统机器学习方法和零样本大语言模型，分别提升12.8%和44.4%。该方法还增强了结果的可解释性，能将提取的证据与诊断直接关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿片类药物使用障碍（OUD）在美国仍是重大公共卫生问题，但其在电子健康记录中难以被准确识别，主要因诊断编码缺失且支持证据隐藏在临床叙述中。因此亟需一种高效、准确的方法来实现OUD的计算表型（CP）识别，以支持干预和改善健康结局。

Method: 采用专家制定的18项检查清单作为指导，构建基于大语言模型（LLM）的计算表型框架，并结合优化提示（OPRO）技术，自动从临床文本中提取与OUD相关的支持性证据。通过两名医生对253例患者病历进行人工标注验证，评估模型性能。

Result: 所提出的基于LLM的计算表型方法在测试中取得了F1分数0.774和AUROC 0.934，显著优于基于机器学习的CP方法（相对提升12.8%）以及零样本大语言模型（相对提升44.4%）。此外，该方法能够链接提取的证据与诊断结果，提升了模型的可解释性。

Conclusion: 基于专家检查清单与优化提示的大语言模型框架在阿片类药物使用障碍的计算表型识别中表现优异，具备高准确性与可解释性，具有临床应用潜力，可有效支持精准干预与健康改善。

Abstract: Opioid use disorder (OUD) remains a public health crisis in the United States, yet it is difficult to identify from electronic health records (EHRs) because missing diagnosis codes and supporting evidence are buried in clinical narratives. Accurate OUD identification is critical to support interventions and improve health outcomes. This study developed a rubric-guided large language model (LLM) that incorporated Optimization by PROmpting (OPRO) for OUD computable phenotyping (CP). The framework used an 18-item, expert-identified rubric to instruct LLMs to automatically extract critical text with supporting evidence to determine OUD flags. Two UF Health physicians (GMR and WMG) chart-reviewed 253 patients, including 68 OUD-positive cases. Our LLM-based computable phenotype (CP) achieved the best F1 score of 0.774 and an AUROC of 0.934, outperforming the machine learning-based CP using EHR and natural language processing-extracted variables, and zero-shot LLMs by relative F1 improvements of 12.8% and 44.4%, respectively. The proposed LLM-based CP could link LLM-extracted evidence to OUD phenotyping for better explainability.

</details>


### [42] [Intra-Prompt Parallel Decoding for Common-Context Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05707)
*Theodore Glavas,Nikhita Vedula,Dushyanta Dhyani,Antonios Valkanas,Yilun Zhu,Shervin Malmasi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Intra-Prompt Parallel Decoding (IPPD) is a novel inference method that enables parallel answering of multiple common-context questions within a single prompt, significantly improving throughput by efficiently sharing memory and computation during attention. It uses virtual position IDs and attention mask manipulation to maintain output quality without requiring fine-tuning or architectural changes. IPPD achieves up to 7X higher effective throughput than standard decoding and outperforms prefix caching with PagedAttention in most scenarios.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing methods for common-context question answering (CCQA) process each question independently, leading to underutilized GPUs due to memory bottlenecks during attention computation. Despite batching and caching techniques, the separation of questions across prompts limits speedup potential.

Method: IPPD introduces intra-prompt parallel decoding by combining multiple questions into a single prompt. It leverages virtual position IDs and attention mask manipulation to ensure correct token generation across all questions in parallel, enabling simultaneous decoding of next tokens without altering the LLM architecture or requiring fine-tuning.

Result: IPPD achieves up to 7X higher effective throughput compared to standard decoding, with no degradation in answer quality. It outperforms prefix caching with PagedAttention in most settings, while remaining fully compatible with batched inference even when contexts differ.

Conclusion: IPPD effectively addresses the memory bottleneck in CCQA tasks by enabling efficient parallelism within a single prompt. It offers substantial performance gains without requiring model modifications or retraining, making it a scalable and practical solution for high-throughput question answering.

Abstract: In common-context question answering (CCQA) tasks, multiple input questions share a common context to base their answers from. However, Large Language Models typically generate each answer using an independent prompt. While existing batching and caching techniques help improve parallelism and reduce repeated computations, the separation of questions across prompts limits the achievable speedup, as modern GPUs are underutilized due to a memory bottleneck during attention. We present Intra-Prompt Parallel Decoding (IPPD), a novel inference method that answers multiple common-context questions in parallel within a single prompt. IPPD directly addresses the bottleneck by efficiently sharing both memory and computation during the attention process, as the next token for every question is decoded in a single inference step. IPPD uses virtual position IDs and attention mask manipulation to generate the same output as standard prompting without requiring fine-tuning or any changes to the LLM architecture. Since all parallelism occurs within a prompt, IPPD is fully compatible with batched inference, even when each prompt features a different context. Our experiments show that IPPD delivers up to 7X the effective throughput as standard decoding without quality degradation, and outperforms prefix caching with PagedAttention in most settings.

</details>


### [43] [MedWER: A Reproducible, Model-Free Evaluation Protocol for Medical Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05728)
*Justin Behling*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MedWER，一种用于医疗自动语音识别（ASR）的评估协议和开源工具，其分母为固定且许可证清洁的术语列表（包含19,373个药物、诊断、症状和伤害机制条目），避免了依赖版本化的NER模型或云API。该方法结合固定版本的文本归一化器与短语感知的术语限制词错误率（MedWER），确保评估的可重复性与透明性。通过独立的省级药品福利文件验证覆盖率，并基于真实实体跨度校准匹配启发式算法。在Moonshine~base、Whisper~base.en和MedASR三个基线模型上，使用该工具对两个公开基准进行评估，并报告了基于重采样每句得分的95%置信区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的词错误率（WER）会掩盖临床关键错误，例如将一种药物误识别为另一种，而现有改进方法通常依赖于评估时的NER模型或云API，导致评估过程不可复现且存在版本黑箱问题。因此需要一种更可靠、可重复、专注于医疗实体的评估方法。

Method: 提出MedWER评估协议，采用固定版本的术语列表作为评估分母，结合一个固定版本的文本归一化器与短语感知的术语限制词错误率计算方式，仅将文本归一化器作为版本化组件，通过精确版本控制和黄金测试用例验证。使用独立数据集验证术语覆盖，并以真实实体标注校准匹配策略。

Result: 在多个医疗ASR模型上应用MedWER工具后，获得更准确、可复现的评估结果，有效揭示了传统WER可能忽略的临床关键错误。实验报告了95%置信区间，提升了评估的统计可靠性。

Conclusion: MedWER提供了一种透明、可复现、专注医疗实体的评估范式，解决了传统词错误率在医疗场景下的局限性，推动了医疗语音识别系统的可信评估发展。

Abstract: Overall word error rate hides clinically critical errors: a transcript can be 95% correct and still swap one drug for another. The usual fix weights errors on medical entities, and almost always depends on an evaluation-time named-entity recognition (NER) model or cloud API, which makes the metric's denominator a versioned black box. We present MedWER, an evaluation protocol and open-source tool for medical ASR whose denominator is a fixed, license-clean term list: 19,373 drug, diagnosis, symptom, and injury-mechanism entries projected from public sources. The protocol couples a pinned text normalizer with a phrase-aware term-restricted WER, the MedWER, so the only versioned component is a normalizer dependency held at an exact release and checked against committed golden fixtures. Coverage is validated against an independent provincial drug-benefit file the list was not built from; the matching heuristic is calibrated against ground-truth entity spans. Baselines for Moonshine~base, Whisper~base.en, and MedASR on two open benchmarks are scored with the released tool and reported with 95% confidence intervals from resampled per-utterance scores.

</details>


### [44] [Some Tokens Behave like Magnets: Revealing Linguistic Organization in the Layers of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05743)
*Andrew Liu,Devan Srinivasan,Gerald Penn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究发现大型语言模型（LLMs）中存在一类特殊的向量，称为磁性向量，它们通过吸引或排斥周围词元来组织语言结构。功能词在早期层中表现为排斥性磁体，而随着模型深入，磁性极性会发生变化。在微调任务中，与任务相关的词元会成为磁体，例如问答任务中答案片段的词元在最终层表现为排斥性磁体，几何上将答案从上下文中分离出来。移除早期排斥性磁体严重影响句法任务（如词性标注准确率从91%降至10%以下），而对语义任务影响较小；反之，移除晚期吸引性磁体则相反。该现象在不同架构、规模和层数配置中一致，且具有因果相关性，为无探针方式理解语言模型跨层语言计算的几何组织提供了新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大型语言模型内部词元如何以几何方式组织语言信息，揭示其潜在的内在动力机制，提供一种无需依赖外部探针的分析方法。

Method: 通过分析词元向量在不同层中的方向与分布模式，识别出具有吸引或排斥特性的磁性向量，并结合微调任务、消融实验和跨模型比较验证其作用与一致性。

Result: 发现功能词在早期层中普遍为排斥磁体，模型深层磁性极性发生重组；任务相关词元在微调后显现为特定磁体，如问答任务中答案词元为排斥磁体；移除不同层的磁体对不同任务产生显著差异的影响，表明磁性机制在语言处理中具有因果作用。

Conclusion: 磁性向量是大型语言模型中一种普遍存在且具有因果意义的几何组织机制，揭示了模型在处理语言时的分层动态结构，为理解模型内部计算提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We identify a special group of token vectors inside large language models (LLMs), which we term magnetic vectors, that organize the surrounding tokens by either attracting or repelling them. Particularly, tokens pointing the same way as an attracting magnet are elongated; tokens pointing the same way as a repelling magnet are compressed. Just as physical magnets pull or push away the iron filings around them, these vectors organize their surroundings through two opposing polarities. Moreover, we identify a statistically significant pattern in linguistic category where function words consistently act as repelling magnets in early layers, and we also find magnets consistently reorganize their polarities in unique ways deeper in the model. In a further case study we find this observation may unveil a deliberate, layer-wise organization in how LLMs process language.
  This pattern is consistent across different LLM architectures, sizes, and layer configurations. It is also causally relevant. When the LLM is fine-tuned for a downstream task, the task-functional tokens emerge as magnets. E.g., in question answering, the answer-span tokens become uniquely repelling magnets in the final layer, geometrically carving the answer out of the surrounding context. Furthermore, removing early-layer repelling magnets devastates syntactic tasks (POS tagging accuracy drops from 91% to below 10%) while sparing semantic ones, and removing late-layer attracting magnets does the reverse. We believe this phenomenon warrants further investigation, as it opens the first probe-free path to understanding how language models geometrically organize linguistic computation across their layers.

</details>


### [45] [CrisisKD: Five-Stage Knowledge Distillation for Aspect-Level Sentiment and Emotion Analysis in Crisis Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05757)
*Marko Haralović,Onat Akca,Salih Eren Yücetürk,Minsi Li,Mariët Theune*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CrisisKD框架，通过五阶段师生知识蒸馏实现社交媒体危机事件中情感词目标的细粒度分析，构建50,615条标注数据集，显著提升小模型在方面提取、情绪分类等任务上的性能，且推理成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在危机情境（尤其是健康相关）下，识别情感词汇或短语的目标对于理解公众关切至关重要，但现有方法在跨文化和多语言场景中存在标注成本高、模型效率低等问题。

Method: 设计一个五阶段教师-学生知识蒸馏框架，利用大语言模型（教师）生成方面级标签和推理路径，指导小型学生模型完成方面抽取、句法解析、观点抽取、情感分类和情绪分类任务。

Result: 构建了包含50,615个方面级标签的数据集并开源；学生模型在500条人工标注测试集上，方面提取提升7.9 F1点，情绪准确率提升17.0点，情感准确率提升6.5点；在外部ABEA基准上，相比基线模型，ATE任务提升2.8 F1点，联合任务提升3.8 F1点。

Conclusion: CrisisKD框架有效实现了高质量、低成本的细粒度情感与情绪分析，为危机舆情监测提供了高效可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Identifying the target of emotional words or phrases in crisis situations, especially health-related ones, is important for understanding public concerns across cultural and linguistic contexts. We propose CrisisKD, a five-stage teacher--student knowledge distillation framework for aspect-level sentiment and emotion analysis on unannotated social media data. A teacher LLM generates aspect-level labels and reasoning traces that supervise a smaller student model across aspect extraction, syntactic parsing, opinion extraction, sentiment classification, and emotion classification. Using this framework, we construct and release a dataset containing 50,615 aspect-level labels, together with the annotation and fine-tuning scripts as open-source resources. The resulting student supports end-to-end ABSA and emotion detection at substantially lower inference cost than the teacher. On a manually annotated 500-tweet gold set, the 5-task Qwen2.5-7B student improves over the untuned model by 7.9 F1 points on aspect extraction, 17.0 points on emotion accuracy, and 6.5 points on sentiment accuracy. On the external ABEA benchmark, CrisisKD improves the same-model Qwen2.5-7B ICL baseline by 2.8 F1 points on ATE and 3.8 F1 points on joint ATE+AEC.

</details>


### [46] [Recall Is Not Protection: Evaluating Safety Monitors Against Model Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05797)
*Sripad Karne*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究安全监控器在检测有害提示时的性能，发现标准召回率可能高估了实际保护效果。通过分析模型对重复提示的响应，提出‘可诱发性’（elicitable）有害提示的概念：若模型至少一次响应，则该提示为可诱发。结果显示，监控器在可诱发提示上的召回率比非可诱发提示低0.22至0.38，且被遗漏的提示其违规响应概率是被捕捉提示的2.8至5.6倍。该现象在多种监控配置和模型家族中均复现，表明应基于目标模型的实际响应行为评估监控器性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前安全监控器的评估依赖于召回率与有害性标签的匹配，但未考虑模型是否真的会响应。若模型不会响应，即使监控器漏检也无害；反之，若模型可能响应，则漏检将导致真实危害。因此需要更贴近实际风险的评估方法。

Method: 通过重复向目标语言模型发送相同有害提示，判断其是否至少有一次合规响应，从而定义‘可诱发性’。在固定误报率下，分别计算监控器在可诱发与非可诱发提示上的召回率，并比较两者差异。实验覆盖六种监控配置（包括激活探测、微调文本防护、120B规模策略条件推理分类器）和三种模型家族。

Result: 监控器在可诱发提示上的召回率显著低于非可诱发提示，差距为0.22至0.38。被监控器遗漏的提示，其被模型实际响应的概率是被捕捉提示的2.8至5.6倍。该差距在不同模型和监控方式中稳定存在，甚至在仅依赖文本的独立监控器中也出现。

Conclusion: 标准召回率可能严重高估安全监控器的实际保护能力。监控器的有效性应基于目标模型是否会实际响应来评估，建议未来评估体系引入‘可诱发性’作为核心指标，以更真实反映安全屏障的效能。

Abstract: Safety monitors screen prompts sent to deployed language models, flagging harmful requests so they are never answered. They are evaluated by recall against harmfulness labels, but a catch only prevents harm if the model would otherwise have complied. We measure the difference directly: we sample repeated responses from the target model, call a harmful prompt \emph{elicitable} if the model complies at least once, and report monitor recall separately on elicitable and non-elicitable prompts. Across six monitor configurations and three model families, spanning activation probes, fine-tuned text guards, and a 120B policy-conditioned reasoning classifier, recall on elicitable prompts falls 0.22 to 0.38 below recall on non-elicitable prompts at a fixed false positive rate. The prompts a monitor misses are 2.8 to 5.6 times more likely to be complied with than the prompts it catches. The gap replicates across three model families and appears also in text-only monitors entirely independent of the target model. This suggests that standard recall may overstate the protection monitors provide in practice, and that monitors should be evaluated against what their models will actually answer.

</details>


### [47] [Dynamic Lagging for Simultaneous Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05799)
*Hieu Hoang,Amittai Axelrod*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对级联同时语音翻译中译码器无法控制上游识别器读写调度的问题的解决方案。通过在稳定前缀（即模型完整输出与当前部分源文本共享的最长前缀）和完整句子对上微调一个前缀感知的decoder-only LLM，结合单次强制解码提示，使系统在源文本持续输入时能平稳生成目标文本，实现无闪烁（flicker-free）。实验表明，该方法在保持全句翻译质量的同时，显著提升早期分块质量，并改善了词级别提交置信度的校准性，降低预期校准误差（ECE）。仅需一个训练无关的阈值即可实现最优延迟控制，优于传统的wait-k和目标后缀删除机制，在FLEURS、WMT24++和CoVoST2等多个数据集上表现优异，且在COMET和MetricX评估下均保持优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在级联同时语音翻译中，机器翻译系统无法控制上游识别器的读写节奏，必须基于不断增长的源文本前缀决定何时提交目标文本，这导致翻译结果可能出现闪烁（flicker）现象，影响流畅性和实时性。现有方法在质量与延迟之间权衡不佳，缺乏有效且高效的在线提交策略。因此需要一种能够适应动态输入、保持高质量输出并实现平滑提交的新方法。

Method: 采用前缀感知的decoder-only大语言模型（如Qwen3-8B），通过在稳定前缀（stable prefixes）和完整句子对上进行微调，使其具备识别和利用源文本前缀的能力；引入单次强制解码机制，将已提交的目标文本作为上下文传递，随着新源内容到来逐步推进翻译；使用训练无关的置信度阈值作为延迟控制手段，实现连续的质量-延迟权衡。

Result: 该方法在保持全句翻译质量的前提下，显著提升了早期分块翻译质量，改善了词级别提交置信度的校准性，降低了预期校准误差（ECE）；仅用一个训练无关的置信度阈值即可实现最佳延迟控制，其性能优于wait-k和目标后缀删除等离散策略；在FLEURS、WMT24++和CoVoST2等多个测试集上，无论使用COMET还是MetricX评估指标，均表现出更优的综合性能。

Conclusion: 通过前缀微调与单次强制解码相结合的方法，构建了一个无需额外训练即可实现高质量、低延迟、无闪烁的级联同时语音翻译系统，具备良好的泛化能力与实用性，为在线语音翻译提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: In cascaded simultaneous speech translation, the machine translation (MT) system cannot control the read--write schedule of the upstream recognizer: it must decide, from a growing source prefix, how much target text to commit. We make a sentence-trained, decoder-only LLM prefix-aware by fine-tuning it on stable prefixes---the longest prefix that any translation up to the current partial source has shared with the model's own full-source output---mixed with full-sentence pairs, and prompt it through a single force-decode turn that carries the committed target forward as more source arrives, making the system flicker-free by construction. We fine-tune Qwen3-8B for EN to DE, JA, ZH, simulating the source stream with reference-transcript prefixes. Prefix finetuning preserves full-sentence quality while improving worst-position chunk quality, and it improves calibration of token-level commit confidence, reducing expected calibration error (ECE) on early source prefixes against a stable-prefix oracle. A single training-free threshold on that confidence is the most effective of the three latency controls we compare: it traces a continuous quality--latency frontier that outperforms the discrete wait-$k$ and target-suffix-deletion quality-latency tradeoff mechanisms. The effect holds well on FLEURS, WMT24++, and CoVoST~2 test sets, under both COMET and MetricX.

</details>


### [48] [AtomCite: Verification and Correction of Supplied Page-Level Citations in Multi-Page Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05802)
*Chen Qian,Yimeng Wang,Yu Chen,Lingfei Wu,Andreas Stathopoulos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出AtomCite框架，用于验证和修正文档图像中的页级引用；引入DocCite基准，首个评估系统在文档图像上验证与修正引用的基准；结合真实错误与注入实例，通过双标注员审计确认1909个真实错误；在三个模型家族中，AtomCite在注入基准上达到约93%的二元验证准确率，修复策略将引用精度从34%提升至87-90%，同时保留超90%正确引用；无需训练即可迁移，提升两个开源大模型在五个公开基准上的幻觉检测性能；揭示自动标签噪声会扭曲验证能力评估，依赖合成或自动标签的评估可能误判系统表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法无法有效检验系统是否能验证并修正已附于答案的页级引用，且当前引用准确性评估多基于生成时或文本片段，缺乏对图像中文档引用的验证能力评估。此外，自动标签噪声可能导致评估结果偏差，影响系统排名。因此需要一种可验证、可纠正页级引用的新框架与可靠基准。

Method: 设计AtomCite：解析答案为断言，检查每个断言与其引用页面图像的一致性，并应用确定性修复策略；构建DocCite基准，融合928个人工注入错误与2,468个来自前沿及效率型模型的真实候选错误，经双标注员审计确认1,909个真实错误；使用确定性标签而非LLM判断作为主标签，人类审计作为独立验证层。

Result: AtomCite在注入基准上实现约93%的二元验证准确率，显著优于所有仅依赖OCR的条件（包括计算量匹配的对照组）及先前基于文本的基线；其修复策略使引用精度从34%提升至87-90%，同时保留超过90%的正确引用；在零训练、固定提示下成功迁移至两个7-8B开源模型，提升其幻觉检测性能；人类审计显示，自动标签噪声会严重扭曲验证准确率，甚至反转系统排名。

Conclusion: AtomCite展示了在文档图像中验证与修正页级引用的强大能力，而DocCite为该任务提供了首个可靠、可信赖的基准。研究强调了高质量人工标注在评估系统真实性验证能力中的关键作用，警示依赖自动标签的评估可能产生误导性结论。

Abstract: Large language models answering questions over multi-page documents are expected to cite the supporting pages, yet supplied citations are sometimes inaccurate, and current evaluations score citations at generation time or against text passages: no existing benchmark evaluates whether a system can verify and correct a page-level citation already attached to an answer. We propose AtomCite, an agentic framework that parses an answer into claims, checks each claim against the image of its cited page, and applies a deterministic repair policy. To evaluate it, we introduce DocCite, to our knowledge the first benchmark for systems that verify and correct page-level citations in document images. Built on MP-DocVQA and DUDE, it combines 928 validated injected instances with 2,468 candidate natural errors harvested from frontier- and efficiency-tier models, of which a two-annotator audit confirms 1,909 as genuine errors. Primary labels are assigned deterministically, not by LLM judges, with the human audit as a separate validation layer. Across three model families (Gemini, Claude, and GPT), AtomCite reaches around 93% binary verification accuracy on the injected benchmark, significantly outperforming every OCR-only condition, including a compute-matched control, and exceeding every prior text-based baseline given the same OCR text. Its repair policy lifts citation precision on the injected mix from a constructed 34% to 87-90% while retaining over 90% of correct claims. AtomCite also transfers: with frozen prompts and zero training, it raises the hallucination-detection scores of two open 7-8B models on five public benchmarks above the same models prompted as direct judges. Finally, the audit shows that noise in automatic labels biases measured verifier accuracy and can reverse system rankings, so evaluations relying only on synthetic or automatic labels risk mismeasuring verification capability.

</details>


### [49] [CONDUIT: A Unified Residual-Stream Restoration Framework for KV Cache Reuse in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05821)
*Pengan Chen,Kaisheng Zheng,Liang Hong,Lixia Yi,Jiyue Jiang,Jiayang Chen,Yixuan Wang,Yimin Fan,Xinyuan Liu,Jiayi Li,Zhanqiu Zhang,Yiwen Guo,Yu Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CONDUIT 是一种无需训练的视觉令牌刷新策略，通过残差流恢复统一单图与多图缓存复用，在仅10%刷新预算下达到接近全前填充的性能（97.0-99.5%），在多个VLM骨干网络上表现优异。其核心是基于归一化注意力与预输出值范数代理的查询条件评分，结合图像级相关性增强实现全局选择，显著降低计算开销并提升推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在视觉内容变化时难以有效利用缓存，尤其在前缀改变导致缓存失效时，精确前缀复用失败；而选择性重计算虽可恢复质量但受限于预算且易误选无关高注意力区域，亟需更高效精准的缓存刷新机制。

Method: CONDUIT 提出基于归一化注意力与预输出值范数代理的查询条件评分机制，通过缓存键-查询注意力与可访问的值范数代理对缓存视觉令牌进行排序，并引入图像级相关性放大策略后进行全局选择，实现残差流恢复，无需修改模型架构或权重。

Result: 在10%刷新预算下，CONDUIT 在五个数据集上的平均性能达到全前填充的97.0-99.5%；在MMLongBench-Doc延迟子集上，仅使用13.5%的全前填充浮点运算量，实现2.99倍的首次响应时间加速。

Conclusion: CONDUIT 为视觉语言模型中的缓存复用提供了一种高效、通用且无需训练的刷新策略，能够在极低计算预算下保持高性能，显著提升推理效率，具备广泛部署潜力。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) often answer new questions about recurring visual content, where reusing the key-value (KV) cache can avoid re-encoding expensive visual prefixes. Exact-prefix reuse, however, fails when the same visual content appears under a changed prefix. Selective recomputation can recover quality under a small visual-token budget, but only when the right stale tokens are refreshed. Raw-attention selection can waste budget on high-attention tokens with small value-norm proxy scores and on query-irrelevant images. To address these failure modes, we propose CONDUIT, a training-free refresh policy that unifies single- and multi-image reuse as residual-stream restoration. Building on norm-weighted attention, CONDUIT ranks cached visual tokens using cached-key query attention and an accessible pre-output cached-value-norm proxy, then applies empirical image-level relevance amplification before one global selection. With one image, the coefficient is one and the rule reduces to intra-image token selection. The method preserves model architecture and weights, adding only a single query-conditioned scoring pass at inference. At a 10% refresh budget, CONDUIT achieves 97.0-99.5% of the corresponding full-prefill five-dataset average across three VLM backbones and leads budgeted methods on average; on the MMLongBench-Doc latency subset, it uses 13.5% of full-prefill FLOPs and achieves a 2.99x time-to-first-token speedup.

</details>


### [50] [SinoGlyphBench: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Chinese Glyph-Level Obfuscation in Language-Model Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05843)
*Yifan Wang,Zimu Wang,Suliu Qin,Changyu Zeng,Tong Chen,Siqi Chen,Yijie Lin,Lingyu Jiang,Jionglong Su,Yushan Pan,Haiyang Zhang,Wei Wang,Qiaoyu Tan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SinoGlyphBench是一个诊断基准，用于识别影响标签的关键语义锚点，并在文本和图像模态中创建原始与字形混淆的匹配输入。通过扰动锚点、背景或两者，该设计区分了与内容审核相关证据的破坏与一般表面变化。在对12个LLM和MLLM进行的176,916次配对评估中，混淆使有害内容的漏报率和误报率分别上升6.1和4.7个百分点，四分类准确率下降5.0点。模型保留了75.7%在原始输入上正确决策的结果。全面扰动导致最大退化，仅扰动锚点比仅扰动背景更具破坏性，跨脚本替换在文本模态中尤为困难。结构化输出分析揭示了可见形式读取、意图信息恢复和最终安全判断中的可观察不一致。因此，所评估模型对使用非标准字形书写的中文内容仍显脆弱。资源可在https://github.com/fengshun124/SinoGlyphBench获取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化内容审核系统在面对使用非标准汉字形态（如异体字、变体字）的中文恶意内容时表现不佳，这类内容对人类可读但会干扰模型判断。为系统评估模型对此类攻击的鲁棒性，需要一个专门针对中文字形混淆的诊断基准。

Method: 提出SinoGlyphBench基准，通过识别关键语义锚点，在文本和图像中生成原始与字形混淆的成对样本；设计三类扰动策略：仅扰动锚点、仅扰动背景、同时扰动两者；基于大规模评估（176,916对）量化模型性能退化，并分析结构化输出中的不一致模式。

Result: 字形混淆使有害内容的假阴性和假阳性率分别上升6.1和4.7个百分点，四分类准确率下降5.0点；模型在原始正确判断上的保留率为75.7%；全范围扰动最严重，锚点扰动比背景扰动更致命，跨脚本替换在文本中尤为棘手；结构化输出显示在可视形式理解、意图恢复和安全判断上存在明显偏差。

Conclusion: 当前主流大语言模型和多模态模型对使用非标准字形的中文内容仍缺乏鲁棒性，表明其在处理语义不变但形式变异的内容时存在根本性缺陷，亟需增强对中文字形多样性的建模能力。

Abstract: Glyph-level obfuscation can leave harmful Chinese content readable to humans while degrading automated moderation. We introduce SinoGlyphBench, a diagnostic benchmark that identifies label-critical semantic anchors and creates matched original and glyph-obfuscated inputs in text and image modalities. By perturbing anchors, background context, or both, this design distinguishes corruption of moderation-relevant evidence from general surface variation. Across 176,916 paired evaluations of 12 LLMs and MLLMs, obfuscation increases harmful false-negative and false-positive rates by 6.1 and 4.7 percentage points, respectively, and reduces four-way accuracy by 5.0 points. Models retain 75.7% of the decisions that were correct on the matched original inputs. Full-scope perturbations cause the largest degradation, anchor-only perturbations are more damaging than background-only perturbations, and cross-script substitution is particularly difficult in the text modality. Analysis of structured outputs identifies observable mismatches in visible-form reading, intended-message recovery, and final safety judgment. The evaluated models, therefore, remain brittle to Chinese content written with non-canonical glyphs. Resources are available at https://github.com/fengshun124/SinoGlyphBench.

</details>


### [51] [What if LLMs Ate Their Words: Causal History Effects in Multi-Turn Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05882)
*Jinnan Li,Zheren Fu,Yue Wang,Jinzhe Li,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型在多轮交互中的表现退化问题，发现模型和任务对退化程度有显著影响，且强的一次性表现并不意味着更强的多轮鲁棒性。通过中性化（neutralization）历史回复，可提升平均性能0.027；即使控制长度，短版本与匹配长度版本效果相近，说明并非单纯因上下文缩短所致。Turn Surgery实验表明，63.7%的退化轨迹可通过单轮干预改善，其中近半数二分类任务可实现从失败到成功的逆转。案例分析显示历史变化与下游状态变化相关，但无通用内部表征。结论：助手生成的历史具有主动且选择性的影响，应采取针对性而非统一的历史管理策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已揭示多轮交互中存在性能退化现象，且助手生成的历史会影响后续行为，但其在不同模型、任务、对话轮次中的具体表现机制尚不明确。尤其缺乏对历史内容如何影响模型行为的深入理解，以及是否可以通过干预历史来改善表现的系统性探索。因此，亟需全面评估多轮交互中的历史依赖性及其可调控性。

Method: 采用六类任务与五种模型进行对比实验，比较完全输入（FULL）与逐步揭示多轮交互（SHARDED）之间的性能差异。随后通过中性化处理已完成对话中的助手回复，仅替换历史内容而保留用户消息，评估性能变化。进一步使用Turn Surgery方法逐轮干预，识别关键轮次中的有益或有害影响。最后通过开放权重案例分析，探究历史修改与模型内部状态变化之间的关联。

Result: 中性化处理使平均性能提升0.027（2,973条轨迹），且长度匹配条件下效果一致，表明非长度因素主导；在237个退化轨迹中，63.7%可通过单轮干预改善，48.4%的二分类任务可实现失败转成功；案例研究发现历史改变与下游状态变化相关，但无普适内部信号。

Conclusion: 助手生成的历史对多轮表现具有主动且选择性的影响，不应采取统一的历史管理策略，而应基于任务与模型特性实施精细化干预，以提升多轮交互的稳定性与可靠性。

Abstract: Multi-turn interaction creates a feedback process in which an LLM's previous responses become context for later behavior. Prior work shows substantial multi-turn degradation and that assistant-generated history can affect later behavior. However, it remains unclear how these effects manifest across models, tasks, turns, and inside a model. We study these gaps across six task families and five models. Degradation from fully specified single-turn input (FULL) to progressively revealed multi-turn interaction (SHARDED) is clearly task- and model-dependent, and stronger one-shot performance does not imply greater interaction robustness. We then retrospectively analyze completed SHARDED conversations by replaying the user messages already observed in each trajectory while editing only assistant-generated history. Replacing prior assistant responses with neutral content (termed neutralization) changes downstream min-max normalized performance by +.027 across 2,973 trajectories. On a prespecified length-controlled subset, short and length-matched neutralization yield nearly identical effects (+.069 versus +.068), showing that simple context shortening is insufficient to explain the effect of history editing. Turn Surgery further intervenes on one assistant turn at a time. Among 237 selected degraded trajectories, 63.7% contain at least one beneficial intervention, while most tested positions remain unchanged; for binary tasks, 48.4% admit a fail-to-success reversal. An open-weight case study links behaviorally consequential history changes to measurable downstream state differences, but finds task-dependent rather than universal internal signatures. Overall, assistant-generated history has active but selective effects on multi-turn performance, motivating selective rather than uniform history management.

</details>


### [52] [AlignDiff: Exploiting Model-Intrinsic Information for Better Preference Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05899)
*Peng Lai,He Zhu,Zhiwen Ruan,Dongdong Zhang,Yun Chen,Peng Li,Furu Wei,Yang Liu,Guanhua Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AlignDiff是一种基于模型内在信号的偏好数据过滤框架，通过识别具有明确偏好的样本并优先处理更具挑战性的样本，提升大语言模型对人类偏好的对齐效果。在LLaMA和Qwen两个模型家族及AlpacaEval 2.0、Arena-Hard、MT-Bench三个基准上的实验表明，AlignDiff在所有设置下均优于七个强基线方法，并通过消融实验证明其有效性，且难度驱动的课程学习进一步提升了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏好数据常存在噪声和分布偏移问题，限制了大语言模型与人类偏好的有效对齐，亟需一种更高质量的数据筛选机制来提升对齐效果。

Method: AlignDiff首先利用正向和反向信号识别具有明确偏好的样本，然后根据平均负对数似然差距衡量样本难度，优先选择更难的样本进行训练，实现基于难度的课程学习策略。

Result: 在多个模型和基准测试中，AlignDiff consistently优于七个基线方法；消融实验验证了其有效性；难度驱动的课程学习显著提升了模型性能。

Conclusion: AlignDiff通过利用模型内在信号进行偏好数据过滤和难度导向的学习，有效提升了大语言模型对齐人类偏好的能力，为高质量偏好数据构建提供了新思路。

Abstract: Aligning large language models with human preferences remains a challenge, primarily due to the critical role of preference data quality in effective alignment. Existing datasets are frequently plagued by inherent noise and distribution shifts, which inherently limit model performance. To bridge this gap, we propose AlignDiff, a preference data filtering framework driven by intrinsic model signals. AlignDiff first identifies samples with clear preferences using both positive and inverse signals, then prioritizes the more challenging samples based on the average negative log-likelihood gap, encouraging the model to learn richer information from them. AlignDiff is evaluated on two widely used model families (LLaMA and Qwen) and three benchmarks widely adopted in the alignment community (AlpacaEval 2.0, Arena-Hard, and MT-Bench). Across all settings, it consistently outperforms seven strong baselines. We conduct comprehensive ablation studies to validate the effectiveness of AlignDiff, and further show that difficulty-based curriculum learning improves model performance.

</details>


### [53] [From Narrative to Auditable Forecasts: A Structured Scaffold for Agentic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05905)
*Yuanpu Cao,Yongkang Du,Yurui Chang,Lu Lin,Jinghui Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AuditForecast 是一种用于结构化概率预测的代理框架，通过量化基准模型、模型引导的数据检索和几率空间中的机械聚合，将预测过程从基于叙述的判断转变为具有明确中间步骤的结构化流程。该方法在多个实时预测基准上提升了准确性和校准度，优于市场隐含参考和更昂贵的深度研究代理，在成本-准确性权衡中表现更优，并生成可审计的预测报告，支持事后分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的代理预测依赖于隐式的叙述聚合，缺乏从证据到预测的显式更新路径，限制了预测的准确性和可审计性。

Method: 首先锚定预测于合适的定量基准模型，利用模型引导的数据检索获取基础概率，再通过几率空间中的机械聚合对模型范围外的情境因素进行更新。

Result: 在多个实时预测基准上，AuditForecast 提升了预测准确性和校准度，超越市场隐含参考，在部分场景下优于更昂贵的深度研究代理，且在成本-准确性权衡中保持帕累托最优，并生成可审计的预测报告。

Conclusion: AuditForecast 通过结构化和可审计的方式改进了代理预测，使预测过程更加透明、可验证，并在性能与效率之间取得良好平衡。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly used for live forecasting, where they retrieve up-to-date information and produce estimates for unresolved future events. However, current agentic forecasting often relies on implicit narrative aggregation: agents collect evidence, discuss it in prose, and often assign a probability without an explicit update path from evidence to forecast. This limits both forecasting accuracy and auditability. We propose AuditForecast, an agentic scaffold for structured probabilistic forecasting. AuditForecast first anchors the forecast with a suitable quantitative baseline model, uses model-guided data retrieval to derive a base probability, and then applies situational factor updates outside the model's scope through mechanical aggregation in odds space. This turns forecasting from a prose-based judgment into a structured process with explicit intermediate objects. Across multiple live forecasting benchmarks, AuditForecast improves forecasting accuracy and calibration relative to strong agentic baselines, surpasses market-implied references in several settings, and outperforms substantially more expensive deep-research agents while remaining Pareto-dominant in the cost--accuracy tradeoff. Beyond performance gains, AuditForecast produces an auditable forecasting report that makes forecast construction explicit and supports systematic post hoc analysis.

</details>


### [54] [UniRRM: Unified Reasoning Reward Models Across Languages and Evaluation Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05910)
*Peng Lai,Yichao Du,Junchao Wu,Weibo Gao,Linan Yue,Longyue Wang,Weihua Luo,Derek F. Wong,Guanhua Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MixReward多语言数据集和UniRRM统一推理奖励模型，解决现有奖励模型在开放任务中评估不一致、可解释性差及多语言支持有限的问题。UniRRM通过分阶段推理链动态生成任务通用与指令特定的评估标准，实现细粒度、输入自适应判断，并保持跨语言一致性。实验表明，UniRRM-8B和UniRRM-14B性能接近同规模模型最先进水平，且对未见评估范式有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习中的奖励模型依赖昂贵的LLM评判系统或不透明的标量模型，在开放任务中面临可靠性、可解释性和多语言支持不足的问题。特别是静态评估标准、碎片化评估方式限制了模型泛化能力。

Method: 构建大规模多语言数据集MixReward（涵盖6个领域、103种语言），并设计UniRRM统一推理奖励模型。该模型采用分阶段推理链机制，动态生成任务通用与指令相关的评估标准，支持多种评估范式（成对与列表式）及多语言输入，提升评估的灵活性与一致性。

Result: UniRRM-8B与UniRRM-14B在多个基准测试中表现接近同类规模模型的最先进水平，且在未见过的评估范式上仍具有效性；消融实验验证了其可靠性和设计优势。

Conclusion: UniRRM结合多语言数据与动态推理机制，为开放任务中的强化学习提供了可解释、跨语言、自适应的奖励评估方案，显著提升了奖励模型的实用性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) excels on tasks with verifiable rewards, but in open-ended tasks, the reliability of reward models remains a key challenge. Existing solutions either depend on costly proprietary LLM-as-a-Judge systems or opaque scalar reward models that lack interpretability. Recent works on generative reward models offer a promising alternative, but they remain constrained by static evaluation criteria, fragmented evaluation paradigms, and limited multilingual support. To address these challenges, we introduce \textbf{MixReward}, a large-scale multilingual dataset spanning six domains and 103 languages, containing both pairwise and listwise data, and propose \textbf{UniRRM}, a unified reasoning reward model supporting multiple languages and evaluation paradigms. UniRRM uses a staged reasoning chain to dynamically generate task-generic and instruction-specific criteria, enabling fine-grained, input-adaptive judgments while maintaining consistency across languages. Experiments demonstrate that UniRRM-8B and UniRRM-14B achieve performance close to the state-of-the-art for models of comparable size across multiple benchmarks, and are effective for unseen evaluation paradigms. In addition, ablation studies validate the reliability and effectiveness of UniRRM.

</details>


### [55] [Neuron-Guided Fine-Tuning: Unlocking Efficient Alignment Mechanisms for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05913)
*Zeyu Wu,Junchao Wu,Shudong Liu,Runzhe Zhan,Xin Chen,Shu Yang,Yichao Du,Longyue Wang,Weihua Luo,Jinsong Su,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Neuron-Guided Fine-Tuning（NGFT）框架，利用神经元激活模式作为统一代理，整合数据选择、参数更新和知识保留，通过自适应任务特定神经元选择、基于激活的数据选择和神经元激活对齐机制，在效率和性能上显著优于现有方法，并有效缓解灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监督微调方法（如全参数微调）面临参数冗余、数据质量不一致和灾难性遗忘等问题，且当前方法通常孤立处理这些问题，缺乏统一的优化信号来协调整个微调流程。

Method: NGFT框架包含三个协同机制：(1) 自适应任务特定神经元选择，通过单次前向传播识别关键神经元以集中更新并减少冗余；(2) 基于激活的数据选择，优先选择能最大化对关键神经元贡献的信息密集样本；(3) 神经元激活对齐，一种新型损失函数，将激活锚定在预训练状态，深化表示学习并保留通用知识。

Result: 在三种模型上跨领域特定和通用基准的实验表明，NGFT在效率和性能上显著优于主流微调方法，同时有效缓解灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: NGFT通过神经元激活模式实现微调生命周期的统一优化，为高效、稳健的微调提供了新范式。

Abstract: Existing Supervised Fine-Tuning paradigms, particularly Full Parameter Fine-Tuning are often plagued by parameter redundancy, inconsistent data quality, and catastrophic forgetting, which current methods typically address in isolation and lack a unified optimization signal to bridge data selection, parameter updates, and knowledge preservation. To address this, we propose Neuron-Guided Fine-Tuning (NGFT), a holistic framework that leverages neuron activation patterns as a universal proxy to unify the fine-tuning lifecycle. NGFT operates via three synergistic mechanisms: (1) Adaptive Task-Specific Neuron Selection, which identifies essential neurons in a single forward pass to concentrate updates and reduce redundancy; (2) Activation-Based Data Selection, which prioritizes information-dense samples that maximize contribution to key neurons; and (3) Neuron Activation Alignment, a novel loss function that anchors activations to pre-trained states, deepening representation learning and preserving general knowledge. Experimental results across three models across both domain-specific and general benchmarks demonstrate that NGFT significantly outperforms existing mainstream fine-tuning methods in both efficiency and performance, while effectively mitigating catastrophic forgetting.

</details>


### [56] [Solving versus Verifying: Catching Contradictions in Tax Reasoning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05928)
*Albert Sadowski,Jarosław A. Chudziak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大型语言模型在结构良好的税务案例中准确率超过90%，但面对缺失或矛盾事实的缺陷输入时表现不佳。尽管最强模型在缺失事实时会放弃回答，却常忽略注入的矛盾，以高置信度输出原答案。然而，当被要求验证输入时，这些模型能有效识别多数矛盾。通过引入一个简单的矛盾检测机制（一次额外的验证调用），可显著提升对矛盾情况的处理能力，仅牺牲约5个百分点的正常准确率，且无需训练或外部工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估大型语言模型在真实法律场景中的可靠性，尤其是面对缺陷输入时的表现；揭示现有基准测试无法反映模型在实际应用中的鲁棒性问题，并提出低成本、高效的方法来增强模型的自我校验能力。

Method: 在基于SARA的税务案例上，对六种近期模型进行缺失事实和矛盾事实的扰动实验；比较模型在求解与验证任务中的表现；设计并集成一个基于模型自身验证结果的‘矛盾门’机制，实现自动拒绝矛盾输入。

Result: 最强模型在缺失事实时会放弃回答，但在矛盾输入下仍会返回原答案（63-76%概率）；而当用于验证时，模型能有效检测多数矛盾；引入矛盾门后，所有模型均显著提升对矛盾输入的拒答能力，干净输入准确率下降不超过5个百分点。

Conclusion: 高准确率不能代表模型的全面可靠性；模型在求解任务中遗漏的缺陷可通过一次低成本的自我验证调用有效补救，证明自检机制是提升系统可信度的关键。

Abstract: Large language models now compute correct tax liabilities on over 90% of well-formed cases in statutory benchmarks, which makes them candidates for the tax-advisory and compliance systems that consume such an answer directly. Real legal inputs, however, are frequently defective: required facts are missing, or stated facts contradict one another. Accuracy on clean benchmarks says nothing about how a model behaves then, and a system that computes straight through a defective input returns a confident number with no sign that anything is wrong. This raises two questions: does a model asked to solve a case abstain when the input is defective, and when it does not, can the same model catch the defect when asked instead to verify the input? We study six recent models on SARA-derived tax cases under missing-fact and contradictory-fact perturbations. The strongest models abstain when a fact is missing but compute through injected contradictions, returning the clean-input answer 63-76% of the time with no signal of the conflict; asked instead to verify the same input, they flag most of those contradictions. We wire that verification call into a simple contradiction gate: one extra call that abstains when the model reports a conflict. Across all six models it recovers most of the missed contradiction abstention at a clean-accuracy cost of at most about 5 percentage points, with no training and no external tooling. High accuracy on well-formed inputs is therefore an incomplete measure of reliability, and the detection the solver misses is cheaply recoverable with a single self-check.

</details>


### [57] [SurveyAgent-HKA: A multi-agent framework for scientific survey generation with LLMs and human knowledge augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05938)
*Tong Bao,Mir Tafseer Nayeem,Yi Zhao,Davood Rafiei,Chengzhi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SurveyAgent-HKA 是一个用于科学综述生成的多智能体框架，通过整合已发表综述和同行评审意见中的知识，改进了端到端的综述生成过程。该框架将任务分解为多个由大语言模型驱动的子任务：从多源检索相关论文并聚类识别关键主题以构建初始大纲；利用人类撰写的综述大纲进行优化；基于优化后的大纲重新检索与重排序论文以选择高质量内容进行撰写；最后结合同行评审中常见的批评点指导修订，完成最终综述。实验表明，该方法在引用质量、结构一致性和内容质量上均优于主流基线，且在时间和成本方面高效，适用于更广泛的AI辅助科研写作场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有综述生成方法通常仅依赖单一数据源（如arXiv）并使用单次LLM调用，导致参考文献覆盖有限，且无法模拟专家撰写过程中反复修订的关键环节，影响综述质量。因此亟需一种能融合外部知识与专家反馈的系统性框架来提升生成综述的可靠性与专业性。

Method: SurveyAgent-HKA采用多智能体架构，分阶段处理综述生成任务：1）多源文献检索与主题聚类生成初始大纲；2）引入已有高质量人工综述的大纲进行结构优化；3）基于优化大纲重新检索并重排序相关论文，确保内容聚焦；4）分析同行评审评论，提取常见问题，用于指导内容修订与完善。各阶段均由大语言模型驱动的智能体协同完成。

Result: 在两个不同领域上的实验结果表明，SurveyAgent-HKA在引用质量、结构一致性与内容质量三项指标上显著优于主流基线方法。同时，该框架在计算效率和成本控制方面表现良好，具备实际部署潜力，可广泛应用于AI辅助科研写作场景。

Conclusion: SurveyAgent-HKA通过融合多源知识与专家反馈机制，有效提升了科学综述生成的质量与可信度，实现了从自动化生成向准专家级写作的跃迁，为未来智能化科研辅助工具的发展提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Automatic scientific survey generation has become an important task in scientific document processing. The common approach of retrieving literature from a single source (e.g., arXiv) and generating surveys through a one-pass large language model (LLM) call often leads to limited reference coverage and, more importantly, fails to replicate the expert-driven revision process that is crucial for writing high-quality surveys. In this paper, we introduce SurveyAgent-HKA, a multi-agent framework that improves end-to-end scientific survey generation by incorporating knowledge derived from published surveys and peer-review comments. The framework decomposes survey generation into well-defined sub-tasks handled by LLM-powered agent. It first retrieves relevant papers from multiple sources and identifies key topics through clustering to construct an initial outline, which is then refined using outlines from related human-written surveys. Based on the refined outline, topic-focused papers are retrieved and re-ranked to select for drafting a well-grounded survey. Then, we identify common issues raised by experts in peer-review comments from published surveys to guide the revisions and finalize the survey. Experiments on two domains show that our approach outperforms mainstream baselines in citation quality, structural consistency, and content quality. Furthermore, our framework is efficient in both time and cost, making it a practical solution for broader AI-assisted scientific writing applications.

</details>


### [58] [The Blindness of Document-Level Translation Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05949)
*Ahrii Kim,Vilém Zouhar,Chanjun Park,Seong-heum Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究测试了文档级机器翻译评估中‘完整文档呈现能引发文档级判断’这一假设，通过引入混合（MIX）条件（即从不同系统拼接段落以破坏段间一致性），发现即使在不一致的情况下，人工评分、系统排名和错误标注与连贯文档无显著差异。尽管评阅者能识别出连贯文本的统一性（87.3%正确率），但其评分行为并未反映这种感知差异。问题不在于评分不准，而在于当前文档级评估协议可能并未真正测量其所意图衡量的内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证文档级机器翻译评估中，完整文档呈现是否真能引发真正的文档级判断；质疑现有评估协议是否有效捕捉系统表现的真实差异。

Method: 采用反事实实验设计（MIX条件），将来自不同系统的段落组合成文档，保持文档级呈现但破坏段间一致性；对比连贯与不连贯文档在18,420次专家英语到韩语标注中的评分结果，并分析14种自动指标的表现。

Result: 在专家评分、系统排名和错误标注上，连贯与不连贯文档之间无统计差异；评阅者虽能感知连贯性（87.3%识别率），但评分未体现该感知。

Conclusion: 文档呈现方式改变了评阅过程，但未反映在最终输出中；当前文档级评估协议可能无法准确衡量系统真实表现，资源投入或未能达成预期目标。

Abstract: Document-level machine translation (MT) evaluation extends segment-level protocols by presenting full documents to annotators, on the assumption that such presentation elicits document-level judgments. We test this assumption with a counterfactual condition (MIX) in which each document combines segments drawn from different systems, preserving document-level presentation while breaking cross-segment consistency. Across 18,420 expert Englis-to-Korean annotations and 14 automatic metrics, scores, system rankings, and error annotations are statistically equivalent between coherent and incoherent documents. Perception does not explain this: shown matched passages, raters identify the coherent one as the work of a single translator in 87.3% of trials. Document presentation does change how annotators work, but that change does not reach the recorded output. What is blind is the protocol, not the annotator. The concern is not that scores fall short, but that the resources invested in document-level systems, metrics, and annotation may not be measuring what they are intended to measure.

</details>


### [59] [Beyond Cross-Lingual Transfer: Benchmarking Propagation Boundaries in Multilingual LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05976)
*Pengyang Shao,Chuanpeng Lu,Wei Qin,Yanzheng Jin,Xiaohao Liu,Xi Ai,Kenji Kawaguchi,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 CLLPU 多语言大模型遗忘基准，区分跨语言传播与语言边界控制问题，揭示现有方法在通用遗忘与语言限定遗忘中的失败模式，并强调传播控制是多语言大模型遗忘的核心挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法无法区分多语言场景下知识遗忘的不足与过度传播，亟需一个能精确衡量跨语言传播与语言边界控制的基准。

Method: 构建 CLLPU 基准，采用目标引导主题配对、模式感知关系匹配和双锚点多语言翻译，生成 800 对知识单元与 72,000 个问答实例，覆盖十种语言，涵盖通用目标遗忘与语言条件遗忘两种设置。

Result: 六种代表性方法在 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 上测试显示：通用抑制时遗忘不彻底，语言限定时知识传播超出预期边界；通用多语言能力可能掩盖邻近知识的损害。

Conclusion: 传播控制是多语言大模型遗忘的关键挑战，需在设计与评估中加以重视。CLLPU 及其构建流程已公开。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) unlearning aims to suppress target knowledge while preserving general capabilities. In multilingual settings, unlearning must additionally propagate within its intended linguistic scope. However, existing evaluations mainly measure cross-lingual transfer and cannot distinguish insufficient from excessive propagation. We introduce CLLPU (Cross-Lingual and Language-Bound Protocol for LLM Unlearning), a multilingual benchmark that formulates this problem through two settings: common-goal forgetting, where target knowledge should be suppressed across all languages, and language-conditioned forgetting, where suppression should remain confined to a designated language. CLLPU combines goal-guided topic pairing, schema-aware relation matching, and dual-anchor multilingual translation to construct 800 matched knowledge-unit pairs and 72,000 QA instances across ten languages. Experiments with six representative methods on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct reveal opposite failure modes: forgetting remains incomplete when universal suppression is required, yet spreads beyond the intended boundary when language-conditioned confinement is required. We further find that general multilingual utility can conceal damage to neighbor knowledge. These findings establish propagation control as a central challenge for multilingual LLM unlearning. We publicly release CLLPU together with its construction pipeline.

</details>


### [60] [Alignment by Stereotyping: How LLMs Sacrifice Individual Distinctiveness for Cultural Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05993)
*Qishuai Zhong,Zongmin Li,Siqi Fan,Aixin Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了大型语言模型在个性化交互中使用用户人口统计信息进行文化适应的策略，发现尽管能提升价值对齐准确率，但会系统性地抹除个体独特性，导致模型将回答推向群体中心点，即‘通过刻板印象对齐’。实验显示顶尖模型在压缩个体差异方面远超人类基准，家庭内尺度放大了这一权衡并削弱了文化理解深度。通过合成对话数据集和真实对话验证，分散对话中的人口统计信号可部分抑制原型检索，但需更大规模复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究当前大语言模型基于人口统计信息进行个性化适配是否真正服务于个体用户，还是通过消除个体差异来提升准确率，揭示其潜在的刻板印象风险与个体性损失。

Method: 基于世界价值观调查数据，分析七种模型（包括前沿GPT-5.1）在人口统计条件下的表现；采用10,000次置换检验评估模型对个体差异的压缩程度；利用家庭内尺度扩展分析文化理解变化；构建并验证合成对话数据集，结合PRISM真实对话数据测试人口统计信号分布的影响。

Result: 大多数模型在人口统计条件下提升了价值对齐准确率，但显著压缩个体差异，表现出‘对齐于刻板印象’的行为模式；顶尖模型在个体压缩上远超人类水平；家庭内尺度加剧该问题并降低文化理解；分散人口统计信号可部分缓解原型检索偏差，但需进一步验证。

Conclusion: 以人口统计信息为基础的个性化策略虽提升模型准确性，却以牺牲个体独特性为代价，导致系统性刻板印象。未来个性化应更注重保留个体差异，避免群体中心化对齐。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed for personalized interaction, and demographic conditioning via user profiles is a widely adopted strategy for cultural adaptation. We ask whether this approach genuinely serves individual users or achieves accuracy by erasing individual distinctiveness. Studying seven models including frontier GPT-5.1 on the World Values Survey, we find that demographic profiles improve value alignment accuracy for most models, but at a systematic cost to individuality. That is, models pull responses toward demographic group centroids rather than preserving individual differences, a behavioral pattern we term alignment by stereotyping. Permutation tests (10,000 permutations, six demographic attributes, seven models) certify that top-performing models compress individuals far above the human baseline; within-family scaling amplifies this tradeoff while degrading intrinsic cultural understanding. Using a synthetic dialogue dataset validated on real human-chatbot conversations from PRISM (Kirk et al., 2024), we further show that distributing demographic signals across conversational turns partially suppresses prototype retrieval compared to compact demographic labels, a finding validated on real conversations via PRISM but requiring replication at larger scale.

</details>


### [61] [ModularPhaseNet: Finite-Cyclic Phase Geometry for Computable Semantic Hierarchy, Direction, and Context Consistency in Standard Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06000)
*Kiyotaka Kasubuchi,Kazuo Fukiya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ModularPhaseNet 是对 QuantumPhaseNet 中连续复相位几何的可计算离散化，将相位通过模运算映射到有限域中的循环子群，利用群乘法实现相位组合，引入商滤链层次与群值连接模块，以实值偏置形式融入标准 Transformer。训练采用实群代数分布或直通 Gumbel-Softmax，推理使用精确模幂和预计算表，避免量子硬件与复数运算。理论证明了量化失真界、商诱导划分嵌套性、规范不变性及平连接的离散可积性，并保证注意力输出有界。核心假设是这些离散不变量能提升层次结构恢复、话语对齐、矛盾检测与幻觉风险预测性能，实验尚未执行，无实证结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在保持标准 Transformer 架构的同时，引入具有数学精确性的离散相位表示机制，以提升模型对语义层次、上下文一致性及逻辑关系的建模能力，同时避免复杂的量子计算与复数运算，实现高效且可解释的模型设计。

Method: 提出 ModularPhaseNet 框架，包含三个新组件：有限相位编码器（将相位表示为模 p 循环子群元素）、商滤链层次模块（构建层级结构）、群值连接模块（表示方向性与一致性）。通过模幂运算与预计算表实现精确推理，训练阶段使用实群代数或 Gumbel-Softmax 近似。所有操作均基于整数运算，不依赖复数矩阵乘法或离散对数计算。

Result: 理论上证明了量化失真上界、商诱导划分的嵌套性、规范不变性、平连接的离散可积性以及注意力输出的有界性。尽管未报告实验结果，但预期该方法在层次恢复、话语对齐、矛盾检测和幻觉风险预测方面表现更优。

Conclusion: ModularPhaseNet 为 Transformer 提供了一种基于离散群结构的精确相位建模方式，兼具数学严谨性与计算可行性，在无需复杂硬件支持的前提下，有望增强模型的语义理解与逻辑推理能力，其有效性有待后续预注册实验验证。

Abstract: We propose ModularPhaseNet, a classical and integer-computable discretization of the continuous complex phase geometry introduced in QuantumPhaseNet. The real-valued hidden states of a standard Transformer are retained, while only an auxiliary phase channel is quantized into a cyclic subgroup G = <g> of order q | (p-1) in the multiplicative group of F_p. A continuous phase e^{i phi} is represented by z = g^a mod p; phase composition becomes group multiplication, relative phase becomes group division, conceptual hierarchy is induced by a filtration of cyclic quotients, semantic direction is represented by oriented relative group elements, and contextual consistency is measured by gauge-invariant cycle holonomy. The method introduces three components into an otherwise standard Transformer: a finite-phase encoder, a quotient-filtration hierarchy module, and a group-valued connection module. Their outputs enter self-attention as real-valued bias terms. Training uses distributions in the real group algebra or straight-through Gumbel-Softmax, whereas inference uses exact modular exponentiation and precomputed tables. No quantum hardware, complex-valued matrix multiplication, or discrete-logarithm computation is required. We prove quantization-distortion bounds, nesting of quotient-induced partitions, gauge invariance, a discrete integrability result for flat connections, and boundedness of the resulting attention output. The central empirical hypothesis is that these exact discrete invariants improve hierarchy recovery, discourse alignment, contradiction detection, and calibrated hallucination-risk prediction under a controlled compute budget. This paper reports the theory together with a pre-registered evaluation plan; the experiments described in Section 14 have not yet been carried out, and no empirical result is claimed here.

</details>


### [62] [Tri-PvP: Exposing Modality Bias in Omni-Modal Large Language Models through Perceptual-Propositional Evidence Conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06011)
*Yen-Ting Piao,Shu-Yun Chen,Chin-Hui Chu,Chun-Wei Chen,Shih-Yun Shan Kuan,Hung-yi Lee,Yun-Nung Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Tri-PvP基准，评估多模态大模型在视觉、音频和文本冲突下的模态偏差，发现视觉偏向普遍且存在感知与命题信号间的不对称偏差，早期表征中即可解码模态偏差，难以通过表面干预缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准混淆了感知信号与命题信号，导致模态偏差无法清晰归因，亟需分离证据形式以准确评估模态偏差。

Method: 构建8000样本的三模态冲突基准Tri-PvP，涵盖视觉与音频的感知及命题形式，评估五种OLLM模型，并结合层间线性探测与对比解码分析偏差来源。

Result: 多数模型表现出显著视觉偏差；模型对视觉更倾向感知信号，对音频更倾向命题信号，且模态偏差在早期表示层即已可解码，难以完全缓解。

Conclusion: 当前多模态模型存在系统性模态偏差，尤其在证据形式上呈现不对称性，需发展深层干预策略以有效缓解。

Abstract: Omni-modal large language models (OLLMs) jointly process vision, audio, and text, yet their modality bias under cross-modal conflict remains underexplored. Existing benchmarks conflate two distinct forms of evidence within a single modality: perceptual signals (e.g., a photograph or recording of a dog) and propositional signals (e.g., the declarative claim "this is a dog"), such that any measured modality bias is inherently confounded with evidence-form bias, precluding clean attribution to either source. To address this, we introduce Tri-PvP, an 8,000-sample tri-modal conflict benchmark crossing vision, audio, and text, where vision and audio each take perceptual or propositional form. Evaluating five OLLMs, we find robust visual bias across most models and evidence-type conditions. Crucially, we reveal a systematic asymmetry in evidence-form bias: models exhibit a stronger bias toward perceptual signal in vision but propositional in audio. Further analyses via layer-wise linear probing and contrastive decoding reveal that modality bias is already linearly decodable from early representation layers and can only be partially mitigated, calling for mitigation strategies beyond surface-level interventions.

</details>


### [63] [Factors Influencing the Emergence of Dependency Length Minimization in Neural Agent Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06025)
*Yuqing Zhang,Tessa Verhoef,Gertjan van Noord,Arianna Bisazza*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过基于循环神经网络（RNN）的通信框架，探究了语言使用者在交流中偏好较短语法依赖长度的机制。实验发现，在增量处理压力下，神经代理才表现出一致的依赖长度最小化（DLM）倾向，表明人类认知加工限制可能影响语言结构的形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究依赖长度最小化（DLM）这一语言现象的起源，特别是其是否源于高效信息处理的认知限制。以往的计算模拟未充分考虑真实交互情境，结果不一，因此需要更贴近实际交流环境的模型来验证假设。

Method: 采用基于循环神经网络（RNN）的人工语言学习与通信框架，训练代理在不同认知限制条件下（如听觉噪声、说话者容量有限、增量句法处理）进行语言交流，并观察其对词序选择的偏好。

Result: 在完整语义空间中，代理趋向单一主导词序；在半语义空间中，表现出先短后长的偏好，仅在动词前置语言中符合DLM；只有在增量处理压力下，才出现稳定的DLM偏好。

Conclusion: 认知加工限制，尤其是增量处理压力，可能是驱动依赖长度最小化的重要因素。该研究为理解人类语言偏好如何由认知机制塑造提供了支持，并揭示了构建能体现人类认知偏好的语言生成模型所面临的挑战。

Abstract: Given various grammatical options, language users prefer the word order choice that reduces the overall length of syntactic dependencies, a principle known as dependency length minimization (DLM). The origins of this preference remain an open question, particularly whether it originates from constraints on efficient information processing. Computational simulations provide a powerful approach to identifying the factors influencing the emergence of linguistic phenomena. However, previous simulations of DLM have not examined realistic interaction contexts and have produced mixed results. The present study investigates the emergence of DLM in artificial languages using a recently proposed language learning and communication framework based on recurrent neural networks (RNNs). In this framework, agents are trained to speak and interpret artificial languages and then use these languages to communicate. Using this framework, we study the impact of several factors related to processing limitations in a communicative setting, such as noise during listening, limited speaker capacity, and incremental sentence processing. Our results reveal a complex interplay among these factors in shaping word order preferences in neural agents. Specifically, in the full meaning space, agents regularize toward a single dominant word order, while in the half meaning space they show a short-before-long preference that only aligns with DLM in verb-initial languages. A consistent DLM preference emerges only when agents are subject to incremental processing pressure. These findings suggest that limitations in human cognitive processing may indeed play a role in shaping DLM. Our findings provide insights into the conditions under which neural models replicate human-like preferences and highlight the challenges of designing emergent communication models that capture human cognitive biases in language processing.

</details>


### [64] [Generating Adversarial Texts for Machine Translation via GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06048)
*Florian Zogaj,Jakob Hütteneder,Giovanni De Muri,Federico Villa,Aryan Sood,Vilém Zouhar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于强化学习的可扩展方法，通过重写源文本生成对机器翻译系统更具挑战性的测试用例。利用基于翻译难度的奖励信号和语义相似性、语法正确性和长度近似保持等约束条件，微调大型语言模型。在WMT25上，该方法将平均COMET得分从0.63降至0.48，显著降低翻译质量，同时保持语法正确性和可读性；在未见的WMT19-WMT24基准上也表现出良好的泛化能力。人工评估显示重写后翻译质量明显下降，自然度略有降低，语法变化极小。代码已开源以支持复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准机器翻译评测基准难以揭示系统剩余弱点，传统手动构建测试集成本高，自动化方法又难以兼顾翻译难度与语言多样性。需要一种高效且能生成高质量挑战性测试数据的方法。

Method: 采用基于组相对策略优化（GRPO）的强化学习框架，微调大语言模型，设计奖励函数综合考虑翻译难度、语义相似性、语法正确性及长度近似性，实现对源文本的智能重写。

Result: 在WMT25上，COMET得分由0.64降至0.48，显著降低翻译质量；在多个未见基准上表现稳定，具备良好泛化能力；人类评估表明重写后翻译质量下降明显，自然度仅轻微下降，语法基本保持不变。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够高效生成具有高度挑战性的翻译测试实例，有效暴露当前机器翻译系统的不足，为评测提供更精准、多样且可扩展的测试数据，具有重要的应用价值。

Abstract: As machine translation (MT) systems continue to improve, standard benchmarks become less informative for exposing remaining weaknesses. Traditional methods for creating challenging test sets rely on expensive manual creation or curation, while automated approaches struggle to produce sets with the necessary translation difficulty and linguistic diversity. We propose a scalable reinforcement-learning-based approach for rewriting existing source texts into instances that are more difficult to translate for MT systems. We fine-tune a large language model with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), using reward signals based on translation difficulty together with constraints for semantic similarity, grammaticality, and approximate length preservation. On WMT25, our approach substantially reduces average COMET translation quality from 0.63 to 0.48, while preserving grammaticality and readability, whereas the base model remains at 0.64. Evaluations on the unseen WMT19-WMT24 benchmarks confirm that this behavior generalizes beyond the training data, and human evaluation further shows that the rewrites substantially lower translation quality while incurring a moderate drop in naturalness and only a small change in grammaticality. We release our code to support reproducibility.

</details>


### [65] [Don't Lose Entities from Retrieval to Generation: Dual Entity Recovery RAG for multi-hop QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06065)
*Heechang Lee,Dong-Young Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为DER-RAG的新方法，用于解决多跳问答中因信息丢失导致的实体消解问题。通过在查询分解和生成阶段显式保留实体信息，DER-RAG避免了检索阶段和生成阶段因上下文缺失而导致的错误，无需图结构构建、语料修改或微调，在多个基准测试上表现优于或等于现有强基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳问答系统在分解问题和文档时会丢失实体信息，导致检索失败和生成歧义，尤其是在句子级分割后，代词缺乏上下文支撑，影响模型理解。

Method: 提出双实体恢复机制：双向查询分解以传递已解析的实体，并在生成时为每个句子添加主体实体前缀，确保上下文连贯性。

Result: DER-RAG在三个多跳问答基准上达到或超越现有强基线，包括依赖复杂离线结构的图方法，且无需额外训练或修改数据。

Conclusion: 实体信息在多跳问答中的保持至关重要；通过简单而有效的设计，DER-RAG有效缓解了‘丢失生成’问题，提升了系统鲁棒性和准确性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented multi-hop question answering (QA) decomposes a query into sub-questions and decomposes the corpus into smaller retrieval units such as sentences. Both forms of decomposition improve the pipeline, but we show that both share the same vulnerability, the loss of entity information, and that this loss breaks the pipeline at two separate points. The first point is retrieval, where a sub-question loses the entity resolved at the previous hop, leaving the retriever with nothing to match against. The second point is harder to see, because retrieval still appears to succeed. Once a passage is split into sentences, an isolated sentence loses the context that grounds its pronouns, so even with the correct sentence in hand the LLM cannot tell which entity the sentence is about. We isolate this second point as a distinct failure mode that we call lost-in-generation, and a retrieval-controlled experiment shows that it degrades answers even when the gold evidence is fixed in the context. We then propose Dual Entity Recovery RAG (DER-RAG), which keeps the grounding entity explicit from retrieval through to generation with two lightweight components, a two-way query decomposition that carries the resolved entity across sub-questions and a subject entity prefix attached to each sentence at generation time. DER-RAG needs no graph construction, no corpus modification, and no fine-tuning, yet on three multi-hop QA benchmarks it matches or exceeds strong baselines, including graph-based methods that depend on costly offline structures.

</details>


### [66] [DPH Parser: A Bottom-Up Grammar-Driven Parser for Joint Constituency and Dependency Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06070)
*Hussein Ghaly*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DPH Parser 是一种受 GPSG 和 HPSG 启发的语法驱动、自下而上的无监督解析框架，通过特征化的句法规则构建短语结构，并通过显式头标注推导依存关系。它结合了概率词性标注、递归短语投影和加权解析假设，能够处理真实且部分噪声的数据输入，同时保持解析过程的可解释性。在 UD 英文语料库上评估，其无标记依存准确率（UAS）分别为 53.32%（开发集）和 52.58%（测试集），低于 Stanza（89.12% & 88.67%）和 spaCy（56.91% & 58.59%），但展示了规则驱动方法在真实树库数据上联合生成成分结构与依存结构的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络驱动的解析器虽然精度高，但缺乏可解释性；本研究旨在探索一种兼具透明性与实用性的规则驱动解析方法，以实现对真实文本的可解释性解析，同时联合生成成分与依存结构。

Method: 采用基于特征的句法规则，构建自下而上的无监督解析框架，利用递归短语投影、概率词性标注和加权解析假设逐步构建语法结构，并通过显式头标注推导依存关系。

Result: 在英文 UD 语料库上，DPH Parser 的 UAS 分别为 53.32%（开发集）和 52.58%（测试集），虽低于现代神经解析器，但验证了规则驱动方法在真实数据上联合生成成分与依存结构的可行性。

Conclusion: 尽管当前性能尚未达到神经网络解析器水平，但 DPH Parser 展示了在保持高度可解释性的同时，有效处理真实文本并联合生成成分与依存结构的潜力，为未来可解释性自然语言解析提供了新路径。
