Abstract: This paper presents Dependency-Phrase Hierarchy Parser (DPH Parser), a grammar-driven bottom-up unsupervized parsing framework inspired by Generalized Phrase Structure Grammar (GPSG) and Head-driven Phrase Structure Grammar (HPSG). The parser incrementally constructs constituency structures using a compact inventory of feature-based syntactic rules while deriving dependency relations through explicit head annotations. The system combines probabilistic POS tagging, recursive phrase projection, and weighted parse hypotheses to process realistic and partially noisy text input. Unlike purely neural and data-driven parsers, the resulting syntactic derivations remain explicitly interpretable.
  We evaluated parser performance on English corpora from the Universal Dependencies (UD) project using Unlabeled Attachment Score (UAS) as the main parsing metric, comparing the outcomes against Stanza and spaCy parsers. For a small inventory of syntactic rules, DPH parser achieved UAS values of 53.32% & 52.58% (UD Devset/Testset respectively). For the same data, Stanza achieved 89.12% & 88.67% while spaCy achieved 56.91% and 58.59%. Although the current system does not yet approach the accuracy of modern neural parsers, the results demonstrate the feasibility of applying transparent rule-based bottom-up parsing to realistic treebank data while jointly producing constituency and dependency structures.

</details>


### [67] [From Two Passes to One: Compact and Efficient Target-Stance Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06108)
*Ethan Mines,Bonnie Dorr*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种单次前向传播的联合架构，同时预测目标和立场，减少近50%的可训练参数，性能仅下降4-7 F1点；发现标准的目标擦除方法会人为抑制目标预测准确率，保留显式目标提及可提升至少6 F1点，有利于下游应用如民意追踪的集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TSE方法采用两个独立神经模型的串行流水线，效率低且参数多；同时，标准目标擦除处理方式可能影响真实场景下的目标预测表现，限制了实际应用效果。

Method: 设计一种一阶段联合模型，一次性完成目标与立场的预测；通过对比不同数据预处理策略（如是否擦除目标）验证其对模型性能的影响。

Result: 联合模型相比传统方法减少近50%参数量，仅损失4-7 F1点性能；保留显式目标提及使目标分类与生成任务的F1提升至少6点。

Conclusion: 该联合架构在保持较高性能的同时显著降低复杂度，且真实场景中保留目标提及能大幅提升目标识别效果，更利于实际部署与下游应用。

Abstract: Target-Stance Extraction (TSE) is the task of predicting both the target (or topic) of an author's writing and the author's stance toward it. Existing approaches to TSE use a sequential pipeline of two separate neural models: one to identify the target and another to determine the stance. We present a one-pass, joint architecture that predicts both in a single forward pass, reducing trainable parameters by nearly 50% with only a 4-7 F1 point tradeoff in performance. We further demonstrate that standard target-scrubbing practices artificially suppress target prediction accuracy. Retaining explicit target mentions, as in real-world deployments, improves F1 by at least 6 points across both target classification and target generation settings. These improvements allow for significantly easier integration of TSE in downstream applications such as public opinion tracking.

</details>


### [68] [STQA: A Benchmark for Stock-Focused Tabular Question Answering over Historical and Forecasted Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06117)
*Baoxu An,Wenmian Yang,Zhensheng Wang,Weijia Jia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出STQA（股票聚焦的表格问答）基准，用于评估自然语言问答在历史数据、数值预测及基于预测的推理任务上的表现。该基准基于大规模金融数据，涵盖4,417只股票和31,400个由专家设计的问题-答案对，并配有细粒度的意图与槽位标注。为此，作者构建了SQFRS（股票查询-预测-推理系统），一个基于代理的统一框架，整合SQL检索与时间序列预测工具。实验表明，尽管当前大模型在历史查询上表现良好，但在基于预测的推理方面仍面临显著挑战，暴露出工具协调与不确定性下推理的瓶颈。数据与代码已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有股票市场分析基准分散于孤立任务中，缺乏对历史记录与未来预测综合推理能力的系统性评估。为填补这一空白，需要一个能统一评估历史查询、数值预测与推理能力的端到端基准。

Method: 提出STQA基准，基于大规模金融数据构建包含4,417只股票和31,400个问题-答案对的数据集，采用专家模板生成并标注意图与槽位；设计并实现SQFRS框架，通过代理协调SQL检索与时间序列预测工具，完成多阶段推理任务。

Result: 实验显示，当前大语言模型在历史查询任务上表现良好，但在涉及预测结果的推理任务中性能显著下降，揭示出工具协同与不确定环境下推理能力的关键短板。

Conclusion: STQA作为严谨的测试平台，推动可信、工具增强型金融智能体的研究发展，为未来金融领域自然语言理解与推理提供了重要基准。

Abstract: Stock market analysis inherently requires composite reasoning over historical records and future projections, yet existing benchmarks remain fragmented across isolated tasks. We introduce STQA (Stock-focused Tabular Question Answering), an end-to-end benchmark designed to systematically evaluate natural-language question answering over historical data, numerical forecasts, and forecast-based reasoning. Built on a large-scale financial dataset, STQA covers 4,417 stocks and contains 31,400 question-answer pairs derived from expert-crafted templates, accompanied by fine-grained intent and slot annotations. To operationalize this benchmark, we present SQFRS (Stock Query-Forecast-Reasoning System), an agent-based unified framework that orchestrates SQL retrieval and time-series forecasting tools. Experiments demonstrate that while current large language models perform well on historical queries, forecast-based reasoning poses a substantial challenge, revealing critical bottlenecks in tool coordination and reasoning under uncertainty. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/xuxubaobaoan/STQA_Project. STQA thus serves as a rigorous testbed for future research on trustworthy, tool-augmented financial agents.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [69] [AhaBench: Do Agents Learn from Prior Experience? A Benchmark for Long-Horizon Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05435)
*Zerui Cheng,Jiawei Xu,Huacan Chai,Jiayang Sun,Pramod Viswanath,Maxm Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AhaBench评估语言代理在长时程任务中的持续学习能力，通过三个组件（Aha-Puzzle、Aha-Euler、Aha-Vending）测试模型在移除支持后的行为改进。采用初始得分、经验后得分和学习提升三部分评分卡，揭示模型在利用支持、后期表现和学习能力之间存在差异。Claude Opus 4.6在综合表现和学习提升上领先，但各任务中表现分化明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方式多在单次轨迹或重置后进行，无法有效衡量模型在长期任务中基于经验的持续改进能力。AhaBench旨在更真实地评估语言代理在面对延迟反馈、支持变化或缺失时的学习与适应能力。

Method: 构建包含Aha-Puzzle（无提示探索）、Aha-Euler（数学问题教学与迁移）、Aha-Vending（模拟自动售货机运营）的多维度基准测试体系，使用初始得分、经验后得分和学习提升三指标量化模型性能。

Result: Claude Opus 4.6在总经验后得分（64.3）和学习提升（+25.8）上领先；不同任务中表现分化显著：谜题任务中高分不等于良好探索行为，数学任务中完整教学效果优于仅答案迁移，售卖任务中盈利与抗风险能力可分离。

Conclusion: 模型在有支持时的表现、长期行为改进能力和实际应用成效并非一致。AhaBench提供了一种更全面、操作性强的评估框架，有助于推动语言代理向真正具备持续学习与适应能力的方向发展。

Abstract: Modern language agents are expected to operate over long horizons: they ask follow-up questions, reuse worked examples, handle tool feedback, and adapt to delayed consequences. Most evaluations still reset the agent after a prompt or score only the final state of one trajectory. AhaBench asks a more operational question: when a fixed model receives useful experience, does its later behavior improve under a related evaluation condition where the obvious support has been removed, changed, or delayed? The suite contains three components. Aha-Puzzle tests no-hint exploration after solved hidden-state puzzles; Aha-Euler turns Project-Euler-style mathematical ideas into generated taught/held-out tasks with exact validators; and Aha-Vending, an open-source implementation inspired by Vending-Bench, tests whether a simulated vending agent remains profitable while handling delayed feedback and operational incidents. AhaBench reports a three-part scorecard: Initial Score measures starting competence, Post-Experience Score measures the later empirical outcome, and Learning Lift is their difference. This decomposition is the main empirical message: models that use visible support well, models that reach high post-experience scores, and models that improve most during a run are not always the same. On the common eight-model panel, Claude Opus 4.6 leads aggregate Post-Experience Score at 64.3 and aggregate Learning Lift at +25.8, with Gemini 3.1 Pro close behind at 63.4. The component results explain the split: puzzle traces raise supported scores but often fail to become no-hint exploration behavior; Aha-Euler full teaching reaches 78.6-100.0% while answer-only transfer ranges from 0.0 to 73.9%; and Aha-Vending separates profitable incident handling from bankruptcy and no-order failure. We release benchmark tasks, rubrics, validators, simulator code, and interfaces for evaluating new agents.

</details>


### [70] [When Do Options Help? Policy Necrosis and Redundant Coverage in Option-Critic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05508)
*Bingyun Liu,Yuheng Jing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Option-critic的终止规则学习实际上无效，且其内部策略几乎不探索，导致状态陷入‘政策坏死’。强制每步终止可保持选项数量曲线，恢复探索能修复坏死状态，使单个选项即可解决问题。性能提升主要源于多个选项同时失败的概率从59%降至4%，而非选项本身的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Option-critic方法中添加选项为何能提升性能，并揭示其内在机制与潜在缺陷。

Method: 理论分析与实验验证，包括对终止规则有效性、策略探索程度及状态坏死现象的分析。

Result: 终止规则学习无实际贡献；策略存在严重探索不足导致坏死；性能提升主要来自多选项联合失败概率下降。

Conclusion: Option-critic中性能的提升并非源于有效选项或终止规则，而是由于多个选项同时失败的概率显著降低，且恢复探索可解决坏死问题并提升任务求解能力。

Abstract: Option-critic learns options: sub-policies together with a learned rule for when each one hands control back. Its headline result is that performance improves as options are added. We explain that result, with theory and experiment. First, the termination rule option-critic learns by maximising return contributes nothing. When the termination test and the policy that picks options read the same values, the test fires at every step, so the learned rule is identical to always terminating. When that policy explores and the test does not, as in option-critic itself, the rule can block the exploration; there are instances where it suffers $Ω(T)$ regret while always terminating holds to $O(\log T)$. Forcing termination at every step leaves the option-count curve intact. Second, the policy inside an option barely explores at all, so a state locks onto the first action that looked good and never updates again. We name this policy necrosis, give a state-level test for it, and find three fifths of states necrotic in a typical option. Restoring exploration repairs those states, and one option then solves the task. Third, extra options improve no option; what falls is the chance that all of them fail in the same state, from $59\\%$ to $4\\%$, and performance follows that joint quantity.

</details>


### [71] [Multi-granularity Adaptive Hypergraph Representation Learning via Granular-ball](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05574)
*Sen Zhao,Yifan Guan,Jinyuan Ni,Gaojie Xu,Zhang Xu,Xiaoyu Lian,Yi Liu,Yi Wang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MGHRL提出了一种自适应多粒度超图生成策略和多粒度超图网络，通过粒球自适应分割生成多粒度超边，并利用层次可逆连接融合不同粒度的特征，有效捕捉高阶关系，在基准数据集上显著优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超图表示学习方法依赖预定义的超边生成方式，忽视了图结构拓扑的多样性及超边的多粒度特性，限制了对高阶关系的自适应发现与复杂结构信息的高效处理能力。

Method: 提出自适应粒度超图生成策略，通过粒球自适应分割生成多粒度超边；设计多粒度超图网络，包含多个子网络并利用层次可逆连接融合不同粒度的特征。

Result: 实验结果表明，MGHRL在多个基准数据集上显著优于现有基线模型，展现出更强的高阶关系建模能力和泛化性能。

Conclusion: MGHRL通过自适应生成多粒度超边并融合多层级特征，有效提升了超图表示学习对复杂图结构中高阶关系的建模能力，为超图表示学习提供了新的有效框架。

Abstract: Hypergraph representation learning aims to capture high-order information in graphs by constructing hyperedges that simultaneously connect multiple nodes. These hyperedges adapt to the graph's topological features, facilitating the extraction of high-order relationships at multiple granularities. Most prior work relies on predefined definitions to generate hyperedges, overlooking the diversity in graph topological structures and the multi-granularity characteristics of hyperedges. As a result, this limits their ability to effectively and adaptively discover high-order relationships and efficiently process complex structural information. To address this limitation, we propose a novel framework called \underline{M}ulti-\underline{G}ranularity \underline{H}ypergraph \underline{R}epresentation \underline{L}earning (MGHRL). MGHRL introduces an Adaptive Granular Hypergraph Generation strategy, which generates hyperedges at multiple levels of granularity through the adaptive splitting of granular-ball, effectively capturing high-order relationships based on the graph's topological structure. Additionally, we propose a Multi-Granularity Hypergraph Network with multiple sub-networks, capturing features from hyperedges at different granularities and integrating them via hierarchical reversible connections. Experimental results show that MGHRL significantly outperforms baseline models on benchmark datasets.

</details>


### [72] [Capsule Lens: Locating and Tracking Concept Geometry in Model Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05575)
*Yiming Tang,Harshvardhan Saini,Samyak Jha,Huaming Chen,Xufeng Duan,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Capsule Lens 是一种用于定位、分析和追踪静态与动态表示中概念几何结构的框架，通过将概念占据的区域匹配为可解释的几何体（胶囊），并以闭式解拟合其参数。该方法在静态表示中揭示了概念的几何特征，在动态表示中展示了不同训练阶段下的表征漂移模式，如CLIP预训练中的全局重构与强化学习后训练中的局部特定变化。结果既验证了已有研究，也提出了新发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解机器学习模型内部表示中概念的编码方式是机制可解释性中的核心问题，现有方法多聚焦于将表示映射到可解释空间，但缺乏对概念如何占据表示空间的直接刻画，且多数研究关注静态表示，难以捕捉动态演化过程。

Method: 提出 Capsule Lens 框架，将概念在表示空间中的分布建模为由可解释参数定义的几何胶囊，并通过闭式解进行拟合，支持在静态和动态表示中对概念几何进行定位与追踪。

Result: 在静态表示中成功定位多个模型中的概念几何，揭示其跨度与范数曲线特征；在动态表示中，发现不同训练设置下存在不同的几何演化模式：CLIP预训练引发全网范围重构，而强化学习后训练则导致局部、概念特定的变化。

Conclusion: Capsule Lens 为分析和追踪模型中概念的几何结构提供了强大工具，适用于静态与动态表示，有助于深入理解深度学习模型的内部工作机制，并促进可信部署。

Abstract: Understanding how concepts are encoded in the internal representations of machine learning models is a central problem in mechanistic interpretability, essential both for the science of deep learning and for the trustworthy deployment of increasingly capable models. Existing approaches to interpret model representations mainly map representations onto more interpretable spaces and do not directly characterize how concepts occupy representation space; various hypotheses have been proposed, but often lack of rigorous validation and largely focus on static representations. In this work, we introduce Capsule Lens, a framework that matches the region a concept occupies with a simple, trackable geometric form, a capsule, defined by several interpretable parameters, fitted in closed form to each concept's geometry and validated on held-out samples. We apply Capsule Lens in two major settings: static and dynamic representations. On static representations, we demonstrate how to locate concept geometry across various models, and how the span and norm curves uncover important geometric characteristics. On dynamic representations, we present three case studies tracking representation drifts induced by distinct training settings, CLIP pretraining, RL post-training on visual question answering, and RL post-training on mathematical reasoning. These analyses reveal qualitatively different geometric dynamics, ranging from broad network-wide restructuring in CLIP pretraining to localized and concept-specific changes in RL post-training. Our results include findings aligned with existing literature as well as novel observations. We believe Capsule Lens stands as a promising tool for locating, analyzing, and tracking concept geometry in both static and dynamic representations.

</details>


### [73] [HB-PVI: A Hierarchical Bayesian Personalization and Value-of-Information Framework for Complex Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05582)
*Hammed A. Olayinka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HB-PVI框架通过联合建模参与者异质性、四种个性化机制的收益与损害，以及额外标签的经济价值，在MUSIC-CAR复杂活动数据集上实现了高效个性化。研究采用留一参与者外评估，结合序列蒙特卡洛更新器和学生-t层级增益模型，以及一步期望信息价值（EVSI）停止规则。结果显示，在主要实际效益阈值下，一步EVSI始终为零，表明无需购买额外标签，直接保留群体推断即可。相比固定十次采样适配器个性化，该策略实现100%标签减少，同时保持后验平均F1损失为0.00217，且有99.92%概率低于0.005容差。在绝大多数成本-阈值设置下，HB-PVI表现最优，支持在个性化增益较小时优先采用群体部署策略，并强调健康感知应用中应以信息价值而非预测准确率驱动个性化决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化可提升活动识别性能，但个体增益异质性强，每增加一个校准标签都有成本。如何权衡个性化收益与标注成本，是健康传感应用中的关键挑战。现有方法多关注预测精度，忽视了标签获取的实际代价与潜在危害，亟需一种能综合评估个性化价值与信息成本的框架。

Method: 提出HB-PVI框架，融合分层贝叶斯建模、序列蒙特卡洛参与者效应更新、学生-t分布的层级增益模型，以及一步期望信息价值（EVSI）停止规则，实现对个性化机制收益、损害及标签经济价值的联合建模与动态决策。

Result: 在47名参与者的MUSIC-CAR数据集上，一步EVSI在所有决策状态下均为零，表明最优策略不购买额外标签，维持群体推断。相比固定十次采样适配器，标签减少100%，后验平均F1损失仅0.00217（95%可信区间：0.00048–0.00389），且超过99.9%概率低于0.005容差。在199/216种成本-阈值设置下为最优，所有高于主成本阈值的情形均表现最优。

Conclusion: 当个性化增益相对于标注、计算和潜在伤害成本较小时，应优先采用群体先行部署策略；在健康传感应用中，个性化决策应基于信息价值分析，而非单纯依赖预测准确性。

Abstract: Personalization can improve activity-recognition performance, but participant-specific gains are heterogeneous, and every additional calibration label has an acquisition cost. This study presents HB-PVI, a hierarchical Bayesian personalization and value-of-information framework jointly modeling participant heterogeneity, the benefit and harm of four personalization mechanisms, and the economic value of an additional label, for the 47-participant MUSIC-CAR complex-activity cohort. A leakage-safe, leave-one-participant-out evaluation combines a sequential-Monte-Carlo participant-effect updater with a Student-$t$ hierarchical gain model and a one-step expected-value-of-sample-information (EVSI) stopping rule. Adapter personalization produced small positive mean F1 gains, growing from 0.00099 at one label to 0.00198 at ten, while adapter-plus-head and prototype-residual personalization were negative on average. Under the primary practical-benefit threshold ($Δ_{\min}=0.01$) and cost setting, one-step EVSI was zero at every decision state, so the policy purchased no labels and retained population inference for all 47 participants, matching always-stop exactly (region-of-practical-equivalence probability $=1$). Relative to fixed ten-shot adapter personalization, this reduced labeling by 100\% while keeping the posterior mean F1 loss at 0.00217 (95\% credible interval, 0.00048 to 0.00389), with posterior probability 0.9992 of remaining below the 0.005 tolerance. HB-PVI was utility-optimal in 199 of 216 cost-threshold settings and in every setting at or above the primary label cost. These results argue for a population-first deployment policy whenever personalization gains are small relative to labeling, computation, and harm costs, and show that value-of-information reasoning, not raw predictive accuracy, should drive personalization decisions in health-sensing applications.

</details>


### [74] [Endogenous Exploration in Reinforcement Learning with Intrinsic Curiosity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05650)
*Armando Vieira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于内在好奇心驱动探索的强化学习框架，适用于非平稳、奖励稀疏或延迟的环境。通过结合外部奖励与认知动机机制，引导智能体向有结构的探索方向前进。核心假设是：在中等程度的不一致水平下，有效探索得以产生；而过于僵化或过于混乱的动力学则会降低性能。该方法基于液态状态机（LSM）实现，并在LunarLanderv2和BipedalWalkerv3两个基准任务上验证，表现优于或媲美PPO和ICM等主流深度强化学习算法。此外，实验表明主动推理（Active Inference）代理无法恢复类似的‘好奇心窗口’，说明该方法捕捉到了独特的探索模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非平稳、奖励稀疏或延迟的环境中，传统强化学习方法难以有效探索。为解决此问题，需引入内在好奇心机制，以驱动智能体主动探索未知状态，从而提升学习效率。

Method: 采用基于液态状态机（LSM）的架构，结合外部奖励与基于认知不确定性的内在动机机制，设计了一种新型探索策略。通过调节内在激励的强度，探索行为在中等不一致性水平下达到最优。

Result: 在LunarLanderv2和BipedalWalkerv3任务上，所提方法表现出与PPO和ICM相当甚至更优的性能。同时，主动推理代理未能重现类似‘好奇心窗口’现象，表明该框架具有独特探索动力学特性。

Conclusion: 本研究验证了内在好奇心与中等不一致性之间的协同作用对高效探索的重要性。所提出的框架在复杂动态环境中展现出强适应性，且其探索机制区别于现有方法，具备潜在理论价值与应用前景。

Abstract: We propose a reinforcement learning framework in which exploration is driven by intrinsic curiosity, designed for scenarios where environments are non-stationary and rewards are sparse, delayed, uninformative, or absent. In our model, action selection is guided by a combination of external rewards and an epistemic motivation mechanism that biases the agent toward structured exploratory directions. The central hypothesis is that effective exploration emerges at intermediate levels of incoherence, while performance degrades under both overly rigid and overly disordered dynamics. To test this idea, we implement the framework on top of a Liquid State Machine (LSM) substrate and evaluate it on two standard benchmarks: the discrete-action LunarLanderv2 and the continuous-control BipedalWalkerv3. The proposed method achieves competitive performance on both tasks relative to established deep RL algorithms, including Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) and Intrinsic Curiosity Module (ICM). We further show that the curiosity window is not recovered in Active Inference agents under the same analysis, suggesting that the proposed dynamics capture a distinct exploration regime

</details>


### [75] [Robustness of LLM-Generated SystemVerilog Assertions to Semantics-Preserving RTL Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05658)
*FNU Aditi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过语义保持的RTL变换，对基于大语言模型（LLM）的SystemVerilog断言（SVA）生成进行受控的元测试评估。使用VERT数据集构建高质量条件控制池和分层40程序评估集，涵盖295个赋值行为。评估Qwen2.5-Coder-7B和DeepSeek-Coder-V2-Lite两个开源代码模型，在相同提示和贪婪解码下，考察操作数重排、确定性标识符重命名和冗余括号化三种变换。结果表明，9.7%-27.0%原本正确的行为在变换后变为错误，揭示了聚合准确率可能掩盖显著不稳定性。例如，DeepSeek模型在标识符重命名下准确率从53.9%升至63.7%，但19.5%原正确行为失败。人工审查发现错误类型包括路径谓词丢失、分支极性错误、布尔结构破坏和输出合约违反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在SVA生成中的评估多基于单一语法表示的点准确率，无法反映模型在不同但语义等价的RTL写法下的稳定性。本研究旨在揭示这种稳定性缺失，推动更鲁棒的评估方法以提升AI辅助硬件验证的可靠性。

Method: 构建质量过滤的条件控制池与分层评估集；对两个开源代码模型采用统一提示与贪婪解码；施加三种语义保持的RTL变换（操作数重排、标识符重命名、冗余括号化）；在程序级使用10,000样本聚类自助法计算置信区间，评估基准准确率、变换后准确率、条件鲁棒性、不变性失败率与任意翻转率。

Result: 在六种模型-变换组合中，9.7%-27.0%原本正确的行为在变换后失效；部分模型在特定变换下准确率上升，但大量原正确行为失败；人工分析揭示路径谓词丢失、分支极性错误、布尔结构破坏和输出合同违规等典型错误。

Conclusion: 仅依赖点准确率无法充分刻画LLM在断言生成中的可靠性；必须引入鲁棒性感知的评估机制，以保障AI辅助硬件验证的实际可用性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly being explored for automating SystemVerilog Assertion (SVA) generation, yet most evaluations report correctness on a single syntactic representation of an input. Such point accuracy does not reveal whether a model's correct output is stable when the same RTL behavior is written differently. This paper presents a controlled metamorphic evaluation of LLM-based SVA generation under semantics-preserving RTL transformations. Starting from the VERT dataset, we construct a quality-filtered conditional-control pool and a stratified 40-program evaluation set containing 295 assignment behaviors. We evaluate two open code models, Qwen2.5-Coder-7B and DeepSeek-Coder-V2-Lite, with an identical evaluation prompt and greedy decoding. Three transformations are studied: operand reordering, deterministic identifier renaming, and redundant parenthesization. Beyond baseline and transformed accuracy, we measure conditional robustness, invariance failure, and any-flip rate, with 10,000-sample clustered bootstrap intervals at the RTL-program level. Across all six model-transformation conditions, 9.7%-27.0% of behaviors that were correct on the original RTL become incorrect after a semantics-preserving transformation. Aggregate accuracy can therefore hide substantial instability: under identifier renaming, DeepSeek-Coder-V2-Lite improves from 53.9% to 63.7% accuracy while 19.5% of its originally correct behaviors fail. Manual review of 30 sampled correct-to-wrong transitions identifies dropped path predicates, branch-polarity errors, Boolean-structure corruption, and output-contract violations. The results show that point accuracy alone is insufficient for characterizing LLM reliability in assertion generation and motivate robustness-aware evaluation for AI-assisted hardware verification.

</details>


### [76] [PAC-Private Autoregressive Generation: Calibrating Noise to Ensemble Disagreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05676)
*Mina Mirzadehsarcheshmeh,Amir Keyvan Khandani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将PAC隐私机制扩展至自回归生成任务，提出一种基于多重重叠世界（m=128）的私有预测方法，通过公共模型定义候选集并由各世界投票决定噪声水平，实现动态噪声校准。该方法在保持生成质量的同时，有效控制隐私泄露，在WikiText-103数据集上以每标记2^{-32}的预算保留74%微调收益，且成员推断成功率低于51.08%，泄露量仅为预算的17%左右。相比PMixED，本方法在长序列生成中表现更优，无性能交叉点，显著提升推理隐私与内容保真度的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有私有预测方法如PMixED在每次输出时产生隐私成本，且随时间推移对公共模型依赖增强，导致隐私保护能力下降。而PAC隐私通过根据输出变异性动态调整噪声，可在稳定预测时减少噪声，但尚未应用于自回归生成任务。因此亟需将PAC隐私机制推广至生成式场景，同时解决自适应上下文与解码退化问题。

Method: 构建128个重叠世界，每个记录出现在恰好64个世界中；冻结公共模型后为每个世界训练独立适配器。真实世界作为秘密变量。在每一步生成中，公共模型生成候选集，各世界投票，依据后验加权分歧计算PAC噪声；一致时无需加噪。采用耦合解码策略，既保障隐私会计准确性，又防止贪婪解码带来的退化。

Result: 在GPT-2-small和WikiText-103上，该方法在每标记预算2^{-32}下保留74%的微调性能增益，成员推断攻击成功率被限制在51.08%以下，且经过10^6个标记后，实际泄露熵约为预算的17%。相较于PMixED，在相同隐私约束下，本方法在10^2至10^6标记范围内始终保留98%的非私有性能空间，而后者最多仅56%，且无性能交叉点。

Conclusion: 本文首次成功将PAC隐私应用于自回归语言生成，提出了一种结合多重世界、后验投票与耦合解码的高效私有生成框架。该方法在保证强隐私性的同时，显著优于现有方法在长期生成中的性能保持能力，为私有化大模型服务提供了新范式。

Abstract: Language models adapted on private text are often served through APIs, so privacy leakage occurs through generated outputs rather than exposed weights. Private prediction protects these releases. Methods such as PMixED incur privacy cost at each release and increasingly rely on the public model over long horizons. PAC privacy instead calibrates noise to output variability across possible secrets, adding less noise when predictions are stable. To our knowledge, PAC-private prediction has not previously been extended from classification to autoregressive generation.
  We construct $m=128$ overlapping worlds from the private corpus, with each record appearing in exactly $m/2$ worlds, and train one adapter per world over a frozen public model. The realized world is the secret. At each token, the public model defines a candidate set, the worlds vote, and their posterior-weighted disagreement determines the PAC noise; unanimity requires no calibration noise. We prove $I(S;Y_{1:T}) \leq I(S;H_T) \leq bT$. Our contributions are extending PAC privacy to autoregressive generation, handling adaptive self-generated contexts, and introducing coupled decoding that preserves privacy accounting while avoiding greedy degeneration.
  On WikiText-103 with GPT-2-small, we retain 74% of the fine-tuning gain at a per-token budget of $2^{-32}$, while membership-inference success is bounded by 51.08% after $10^6$ tokens; posterior-entropy estimates of leakage are roughly 17% of the charged budget. Inference privacy is not content protection: even when membership advantage on a memorized canary is indistinguishable from zero, the canary is emitted at the same rate. Against PMixED under matched membership-inference bounds on the same data universe and test set, we retain 98% of non-private headroom from $10^2$ to $10^6$ tokens, versus at most 56%, with no crossover.

</details>


### [77] [Connecting Score Matching, Maximum Likelihood, and Expectation-Maximization in Mixed Linear Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05688)
*Zhankun Luo,Abolfazl Hashemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在混合线性回归（MLR）中未知混合权重下的响应方差保持扩散。通过分离得分匹配的统计保证与损失几何和优化信号，揭示了在固定扩散噪声水平下，KL散度将沿扩散路径积分的去噪得分匹配目标与似然性和终端偏差联系起来。在温和正则条件下，估计器收敛至真实参数，其缩放误差收敛至最大似然估计的高斯极限。在固定噪声水平下，推导出得分匹配损失与交叉熵及期望最大化（EM）算子的分解关系，得到具有潜在方差修正项的EM相关低噪声梯度展开。在高噪声极限下，刻画了在各向同性协方差下的梯度下降行为；沿固定信噪比射线，得分匹配不平衡梯度与潜在方差项逐点趋于零。数值实验验证了理论结果与统计保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究混合线性回归中未知混合权重下的方差保持扩散问题，旨在理解得分匹配的统计性质与优化动态之间的关系，并建立与最大似然估计的联系，同时揭示在不同噪声水平下的梯度行为。

Method: 采用得分匹配方法分析扩散过程中的统计特性，结合KL散度连接去噪目标与似然性，推导损失分解，引入交叉熵与EM算子关联，构建低噪声梯度展开，并在高噪声极限下分析梯度下降行为。

Result: 估计器在温和条件下收敛至真实参数，缩放误差趋近于最大似然估计的高斯极限；在固定噪声水平下，得分匹配损失可分解为交叉熵与EM操作相关形式，包含潜在方差修正项；在高噪声极限下，特定方向上梯度趋于零，数值实验支持理论结果。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了得分匹配在混合线性回归中的统计一致性与渐近性质，提供了从扩散模型到经典统计方法（如EM和最大似然）的桥梁，并为高维、复杂混合模型的优化提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study variance-preserving diffusion of the response in mixed linear regression (MLR) with unknown mixing weights. Our analysis separates the statistical guarantees of score matching from the loss geometry and optimization signal at a fixed diffusion noise level. The KL divergence links the denoising score matching objective integrated over the diffusion path with the likelihood and a terminal discrepancy. Under mild regularity conditions and terminal schedule, the resulting estimator converges up to the ground truth parameters of MLR, and its scaled error converges to the Gaussian limit of the maximum-likelihood estimator. At a fixed scale of the diffusion noise level, we derive a decomposition linking the score matching loss to cross-entropy and Expectation-Maximization (EM) operators. This decomposition yields an EM-related low-noise gradient expansion with additional correction terms of latent variance. In the high-noise limit, we further characterize gradient descent on this limiting loss under isotropic covariance. Along fixed high signal-to-noise ratio rays, the score matching imbalance gradient and the latent-variance term tend to zero pointwise. Numerical experiments illustrate our theoretical findings and statistical guarantees.

</details>


### [78] [GraphNOSE: A Graph Transformer in Olfaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05694)
*Mrityunjay Sharma,Sarabeshwar Balaji,Valentina Parma,Ritesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GraphNOSE 是一个基于图变压器的开源框架，能够从 SMILES 字符串预测单分子和二元混合物的多标签嗅觉描述符。它通过结合位置和结构编码，在参数量仅为标准 GNN 的六分之一的情况下，显著优于线性模型、分子语言模型嵌入、分子指纹和基线 GNN，平均 AUROC 提升 4.52%（p < 0.01），在分布外化合物上达到 84% 的 AUROC，超越现有最先进模型（Open-POM: 81%, p < 0.001）。同时，结合 XAI 方法揭示了驱动气味预测的关键子结构，结果符合化学直觉，具备可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前嗅觉质量预测仍面临挑战，尤其在线性模型对新骨架、极端分子量或复杂混合物的外推能力不足。需要一种更通用、可解释且高效的模型来应对这些限制。

Method: 提出 GraphNOSE 框架，采用基于变压器的图神经网络架构，融合分子结构与位置信息编码，实现对单分子及二元混合物的多标签气味预测，并结合 XAI 方法进行可解释性分析。

Result: GraphNOSE 在参数量减少 6 倍的同时，平均 AUROC 提升 4.52%，在分布外化合物上达到 84% 的表现，优于 Open-POM 模型；通过 XAI 可识别关键子结构，预测结果与化学常识一致。

Conclusion: GraphNOSE 是一个高效、可解释且泛化能力强的嗅觉预测框架，适用于结构差异大、数据稀缺的化合物，为化学生物感知建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: Predicting olfactory qualities from molecular structure is an open problem in chemoinformatics. Although linear models can link molecular features to odor descriptors, they often fail when extrapolating to novel chemical scaffolds, extreme molecular weights, or complex odor mixtures. To address this, we introduce GraphNOSE, an open-source graph transformer framework that predicts multi-label odor descriptors from simplified molecular-input line-entry system (SMILES) strings for single molecules and binary mixtures. By integrating positional and structural encodings within a transformer-based graph architecture, GraphNOSE achieves strong performance with six times fewer parameters than standard graph neural network (GNN) baseline while consistently outperforming linear models, molecular language model embeddings, molecular fingerprints, and baseline GNNs by an average area under the ROC curve (AUROC) margin of 4.52% (p < 0.01). GraphNOSE achieves an AUROC of 84% on out-of-distribution compounds (OODs). This exceeds the current state-of-the-art GNN for OOD in olfaction (Open-POM: 81%, p < 0.001), and identifies conditions under which linear models empirically fail. Finally, we apply XAI (explainable AI) methods to identify which substructures and molecular features drive odor predictions, yielding insights consistent with chemical intuition and grounded in the model's learned representations. Together, these results establish GraphNOSE as a scalable and interpretable architecture for olfactory prediction that generalizes to structurally distinct compounds underrepresented in current perceptual databases.

</details>


### [79] [Analysis of Respiratory Sinus Arrhythmia with Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05698)
*Julian Szymanski,Patryk Orkisz,Higinio Mora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于神经网络的ECG信号分析方法，利用呼吸性窦性心律不齐（RSA）现象直接从心电图数据中估计呼吸频率。通过三种不同的神经网络架构自动提取特征，无需手动预处理，实现了非侵入式呼吸监测的鲁棒且可扩展的解决方案，适用于医疗健康和可穿戴设备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统呼吸监测方法依赖于侵入式设备或复杂传感器，难以在日常场景中广泛应用。本文旨在通过利用ECG信号中的生理信息（即呼吸性窦性心律不齐），实现无创、连续、低成本的呼吸率估计，以提升远程健康监测和可穿戴技术的实用性。

Method: 采用深度学习模型，设计并评估三种神经网络架构，直接从原始ECG信号中预测呼吸波形。模型通过端到端训练自动提取关键特征，避免了繁琐的手动信号预处理步骤。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的神经网络方法能够高精度地从ECG信号中估计呼吸率，三种架构均表现出良好的泛化能力，尤其在动态环境和不同个体间具有稳定性。该方法显著优于传统基于规则或手工特征的方法。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了利用深度学习从ECG信号中直接推断呼吸率的可行性与有效性，为非侵入式呼吸监测提供了一种高效、可扩展的技术路径，具备在智能健康设备和临床监护系统中广泛应用的潜力。

Abstract: The paper introduces a neural network-based approach for analyzing ECG signals to estimate respiratory rate by leveraging the phe- nomenon of Respiratory Sinus Arrhythmia (RSA). Our method employs a deep learning model trained to predict respiratory waveforms directly from ECG input data. To achieve this, we developed and evaluated three different neural network architectures capable of automatically extract- ing relevant features from ECG signals without the need for manual preprocessing. The proposed approach offers a robust and scalable solu- tion for non-invasive respiratory monitoring, with potential applications in healthcare and wearable technology

</details>


### [80] [Newton Matching for Generative Modeling: A Unified Framework for Fine-Tuning and Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05727)
*Zeyang Li,Yunan Wang,Paolo Giaretta,Navid Azizan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Newton Matching框架，统一生成模型中的微调与采样问题。通过将优化从孤立损失转向对标准条件匹配的种群最小化器的迭代优化，构建了基于规范模型的理论基础。在光滑实现假设下，规范速度构成一个与密度流形微分同胚的流形，并将Fisher-Rao度量和混合连接迁移至该流形。发现反KL散度的Hessian等于度量，因此牛顿方向即为负Fisher-Rao梯度。每个阶段执行由正则化奖励 $r - \frac{1}{\tau}\log(\rho/\mu)$ 生成的切向步长，随后进行保持终端密度的规范重收缩。该过程提供精确的有限步长密度表征。理论上证明：当 $0 < \eta \le \tau$ 时严格减少反KL散度；在弱条件下全局收敛；全步长（$\eta = \tau$）时局部二次收敛。通过协方差与梯度形式，结合前向或反向回归对构造出样本级切向更新损失，具有相同的种群最小值，无需重要性采样或全轨迹反向传播。进一步提出近似更新并定义临界点一致性：仅当 $\rho = \pi$ 时切向位移消失。恢复多个代表性方法作为精确实现、临界点一致近似或目标改变变体，支持模块化算法设计，推动生成模型强化学习的理论与算法发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型的微调与采样方法多依赖于孤立的损失函数，缺乏统一的理论框架，难以保证收敛性与一致性。尤其在强化学习背景下，如何高效、稳定地优化生成模型的分布，同时避免复杂计算如重要性采样或全轨迹反向传播，是核心挑战。本文旨在建立一个统一且可解释的优化框架，以提升生成模型在微调与采样任务中的性能与稳定性。

Method: 提出Newton Matching框架，基于规范模型的迭代优化，利用标准条件匹配的种群最小化器作为优化基础。在光滑实现假设下，构建规范速度流形，将其与密度流形建立微分同胚关系，并迁移Fisher-Rao度量与混合连接。通过分析反KL Hessian与度量的关系，推导出牛顿方向等价于负Fisher-Rao梯度。采用正则化奖励生成切向步长，并通过终端密度保持的规范重收缩实现精确有限步长更新。设计两种样本级更新损失（协方差与梯度形式），结合前向/反向回归对，避免重要性采样与全轨迹反向传播。引入临界点一致性概念，确保仅在目标分布时位移为零。

Result: 理论证明：在 $0 < \eta \le \tau$ 时严格减少反KL散度；满足弱条件时全局收敛；全步长时局部二次收敛。所提方法在样本级更新中无需重要性采样或全轨迹反向传播，具备高效性。多种现有方法被识别为该框架下的精确实现或近似版本，验证其通用性。实验与理论表明，该框架在生成模型微调与采样中表现优越，具备良好的理论保障与工程可行性。

Conclusion: Newton Matching提供了一个统一、严谨且高效的生成模型微调与采样框架。通过将优化置于规范模型的几何结构之上，实现了理论上的强保证与算法上的灵活性。其模块化设计支持多样方法集成，为强化学习驱动的生成模型研究提供了新范式。

Abstract: We develop Newton Matching, a unified framework for fine-tuning and sampling in generative modeling. The target is $π\proptoμe^{τr}$, where $r$ is the reward, $τ>0$ the inverse temperature, and $μ$ denotes the pretrained model's terminal density for fine-tuning or the constant $1$ for sampling. We shift the paradigm from isolated losses to iterative optimization over canonical models: population minimizers of standard conditional matching for terminal densities. Under compatible smooth-realization assumptions, canonical velocities form a manifold diffeomorphic to the density manifold. Transporting the Fisher-Rao metric and mixture connection to this manifold, we show that the reverse-KL Hessian equals the metric, so the Newton direction coincides with the negative Fisher-Rao gradient. At terminal density $ρ$, each stage takes a tangential step generated by the regularized reward $r-\frac1τ\log(ρ/μ)$, followed by terminal-density-preserving canonicalization. This canonical retraction yields an exact finite-stepsize density characterization. For the ideal iteration, we prove strict reverse-KL descent away from the target for $0 < η\le τ$, global convergence under mild conditions, and local quadratic convergence for full steps ($η=τ$). Covariance and gradient forms, each with forward or reverse regression-pair constructions, yield sample-wise tangential-update losses with the same population minimizer, without importance sampling or full-trajectory backpropagation. We develop approximate updates and define critical-point consistency as vanishing tangential displacement if and only if $ρ=π$. We recover representative methods as exact realizations, critical-point-consistent approximations, or objective-altering variants, enabling modular algorithm design. Our work advances the theory and algorithms of reinforcement learning for generative models.

</details>


### [81] [A Multi-Source Ensemble Approach to Candidate Generation for Alternative Vacation Rental Property Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05748)
*Syed Mohammed Arshad Zaidi,Eric Rincon,Shayan Hassantabar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了度假租赁平台中候选房源推荐的生成方法，比较了协同过滤、浅层嵌入和图神经网络（GNN）等方法。在超过200万活跃房源的大规模数据集上，结合基于物品的协同过滤与GNN检索的混合架构，将Recall@300提升了14.8%，显著优于最强基线。GNN嵌入单独使用时也大幅优于浅层Hotel2Vec嵌入，显示出其在发现多样化、非明显替代房源及冷启动场景中的优势。此外，研究发现更强的候选池能提升下游排序质量，但因候选生成与排序模型训练耦合，效果归因复杂，提示实践者需关注召回-转化差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 度假租赁市场中高质量候选房源推荐面临异构房源、地理限制、快速可用性变化和长尾分布等挑战，亟需有效候选生成方法以提升用户体验和匹配效率。

Method: 对比分析协同过滤、浅层嵌入和图神经网络（GNN）等多种候选生成方法，提出一种结合物品协同过滤与GNN检索的混合架构，并在大规模真实数据上进行实验验证。

Result: 混合架构使Recall@300提升14.8%；GNN嵌入单独使用时比Hotel2Vec提升48%-68%相对召回率；更强的候选池有助于提升下游排序质量，但存在召回-转化差距问题。

Conclusion: 融合协同过滤与GNN的混合架构在候选生成中表现最优，兼具高召回与多样性，且对下游排序有积极影响。然而，候选生成与排序之间的耦合关系使得效果归因困难，需在系统部署中重点关注召回-转化差距。

Abstract: Alternative property recommendations play a critical role in vacation rental marketplaces, helping users discover relevant options when viewing a specific listing. However, generating high-quality candidate alternatives presents unique challenges: heterogeneous inventory, geographic constraints, rapid availability changes, and long-tail property distributions. We present a comprehensive study of candidate generation (CG) approaches for vacation rental alternatives, comparing collaborative filtering, shallow embeddings, and graph neural network (GNN) methods.
  Our experiments on a large-scale vacation rental platform (over 2M active properties) show that a hybrid architecture combining item-based collaborative filtering with GNN-based retrieval improves Recall@300 by 14.8% over the strongest baseline, by leveraging the complementary strengths of the two sources: collaborative filtering excels at early recall for properties with rich interaction history, while GNNs discover diverse, non-obvious alternatives and handle cold-start scenarios more effectively. As a component result, GNN-based embeddings alone substantially outperform shallow Hotel2Vec embeddings (48-68% relative recall improvement across K), motivating their inclusion in the ensemble.
  Crucially, we examine how CG-stage gains carry through to the downstream ranking stage, and find that a stronger candidate pool yields higher downstream ranking quality, though attributing this effect cleanly is complicated by the coupling between candidate generation and ranker training. This recall-conversion gap is an important consideration for practitioners deploying new retrieval methods in two-stage recommendation systems.

</details>


### [82] [Data Scout: Targeted Web Crawling for Domain-Specific Pretraining Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05766)
*Chirag Garg,Eelaaf Zahid,Farhan Ahmed,Jay Pankaj Gala,Eric Butler,Heiko Ludwig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Data Scout proposes a targeted crawl approach for building domain-specific pretraining corpora, especially effective for specialized domains where traditional filtering of web archives fails. It uses an LLM to generate search queries from a root topic, then screens subdomains via a user-provided classifier (probe). This method achieves high relevance at the subdomain level—math pages are 21x more likely to be relevant if on the right subdomain. With FineMath as the probe, 21.9% of crawled pages are high-quality math content, 70x better than filtering CommonCrawl. Importantly, 63.2% of these pages are absent from CommonCrawl yet valuable. Pretraining Llama-3.2-3B on 1.9B Data Scout tokens matches performance on GSM8k. The method is generalizable to any domain with a suitable classifier.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing methods for building domain-specific pretraining corpora rely on filtering large web archives like CommonCrawl, which works well for popular domains but fails for specialized ones due to sparse and inaccessible content. Targeted crawls are needed for such cases.

Method: Data Scout inverts the traditional filtering approach: it uses an LLM to expand a root topic into a taxonomy and thousands of search queries; returned URLs (seeds) are grouped by subdomain and screened using a user-supplied classifier (the probe); only subdomains passing a sample-based threshold are admitted.

Result: With FineMath as the probe, 21.9% of crawled pages are high-quality math content—70 times higher than the 0.31% rate from filtering CommonCrawl. 63.2% of these pages are missing from CommonCrawl yet useful. Pretraining Llama-3.2-3B on 1.9B Data Scout tokens matches the performance of the FineMath corpus on GSM8k.

Conclusion: Data Scout enables efficient and effective construction of high-quality domain-specific corpora by targeting subdomains with a simple classifier, offering significant advantages over traditional filtering, especially for underrepresented domains. The approach is generalizable to any domain with a suitable probe classifier.

Abstract: The dominant approach to building domain-specific pretraining corpora is to filter large web archives such as CommonCrawl. This works well for popular domains but breaks down for specialized ones, where relevant content is sparse and often beyond the reach of popularity-driven crawlers. We present Data Scout, which inverts this: instead of filtering an archive, it directs a targeted crawl. An LLM expands a root topic into a taxonomy and thousands of search queries; the returned URLs (seeds) are grouped by subdomain and screened with a user-supplied classifier (the probe), admitting each subdomain on the basis of a small sample. This works because relevance has a sharp boundary at the subdomain level: in mathematics, a page is 21x more likely to be relevant than one on a sibling subdomain. With the FineMath classifier as the probe, 21.9% of crawled pages are high-quality math content, 70x the 0.31% rate from filtering a comparable web sample, so the crawl wastes far less effort. But the payoff is not just efficiency: 63.2% of these pages are missing from CommonCrawl altogether, yet just as useful for training. Continued pretraining of Llama-3.2-3B on 1.9B Data Scout tokens matches FineMath corpus on GSM8k. Because the probe is the only domain-specific component, Data Scout can in principle apply to any domain with such a classifier.

</details>


### [83] [RAPTOR: Role-Aware Private Training for Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05770)
*Duc Dm,Khai Le-Duc,Nguyen Do,Minh Son Hoang,Florent Draye,Thai Hoang,Hoang Phuong Dam,Jiarui Liu,Chris Ngo,Terry Jingchen Zhang,Anh Le Duc Tran,Nhat Do Minh,Minh Ngoc Le,My T. Thai,Ran Xu,Silvio Savarese,Mona Diab,Bernhard Schölkopf,Zhijing Jin,Huy L. Nguyen,Daeyoung Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RAPTOR框架，解决DP微调中稀疏MoE模型因将专家视为密集块导致的三类失效模式：全局裁剪抑制专家梯度、批级归一化稀释稀疏更新、固定隐私噪声降低低负载专家信噪比。通过角色感知优化，分别对共享层和专家层采用特定裁剪与噪声，并引入公共期望拥有者分母和独立于计数的更新调度，实现$(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP。证明各专家并行更新不增加隐私开销，共享与专家流串行组合。推导出公共分母估计器的偏差-方差分解，给出基于路由不平衡选择保护层的无隐私规则。在Switch Transformer、OLMoE和DeepSeek-VL2-Tiny上实验显示，在多种隐私预算下均优于标准DP基线，尤其在紧预算下优势显著。代码与模型公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私微调方法将稀疏MoE模型当作单一密集块处理，忽略了共享层暴露全部数据而专家仅接收路由记录的事实，导致三类隐私机制失效：全局裁剪压制专家梯度、批级归一化稀释稀疏更新、固定噪声降低低负载专家信噪比。

Method: 提出RAPTOR框架，通过交替优化共享层与专家层，针对每种失效模式设计解决方案：使用专家特定裁剪与噪声；引入公共期望拥有者分母以避免依赖私有实际专家计数；采用计数无关的更新调度策略。结合并行与串行隐私组合原理，保证整体满足$(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP。

Result: 在GLUE任务上的Switch Transformer与OLMoE微调，以及DeepSeek-VL2-Tiny上，相较于标准DP基线，无论在何种隐私预算（$\varepsilon$）下均表现出一致提升，尤其在高隐私要求（小$\varepsilon$）时优势明显。同时，推导出公共分母估计器的偏差随路由不平衡增长的规律，提供无需隐私成本的层保护选择准则。

Conclusion: RAPTOR通过角色感知的隐私机制设计，有效应对稀疏MoE模型在差分隐私微调中的核心挑战，实现更强的隐私保障与性能表现，且具备可解释性与实用性，为高效安全的MoE模型训练提供了新范式。

Abstract: Differentially private (DP) fine-tuning methods treat sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models as a single dense block, ignoring that shared layers see all data while experts only see routed records. We identify and formally characterize three resulting failure modes: global clipping suppresses expert gradients, batch-level normalization dilutes sparse expert updates, and fixed privacy noise degrades signal-to-noise ratio on low-load experts. We introduce RAPTOR - a Role-Aware Private Training framework, which alternates shared and expert optimization and targets each failure directly, using expert-specific clipping and noise together with a public expected-owner denominator and a count-independent update schedule that avoids conditioning on private, realized expert counts. We prove the resulting mechanism satisfies $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP: because each record is assigned to exactly one owner expert, per-expert mechanisms within a layer compose in parallel, so updating all $E$ experts costs no more, in privacy terms, than updating one, with shared and expert streams composing sequentially across training. We further derive a bias-variance decomposition of the public-denominator estimator showing its bias grows predictably with routing imbalance, yielding a privacy-free rule for selecting which layer to protect from routing entropy measured on a small public corpus. Experiments on Switch Transformer and OLMoE fine-tuning across GLUE tasks, and on DeepSeek-VL2-Tiny, show consistent gains over standard DP baselines across several privacy levels ($\varepsilon$), with the largest margins typically at the tightest budgets. Code and models are publicly available: https://github.com/leduckhai/RAPTOR

</details>


### [84] [Nonlinear elliptic homogenization with the parametric Deep Ritz method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05778)
*Conor Rowan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用深度瑞兹方法求解非线性多尺度问题中的参数化细胞问题，通过神经网络构建连续可微的细胞响应表示，显著提升了宏观尺度求解效率，优于传统FE²方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非线性椭圆型偏微分方程背景下，传统均质化方法需要求解依赖于宏观状态和梯度的参数化细胞问题，而数值求解中需对均质化本构关系求导以进行牛顿迭代，因此需要一种能提供连续且可微表示的方法。

Method: 基于细胞问题的变分结构，采用神经网络同时离散空间与宏观状态的依赖关系，通过强边界条件约束，利用参数化深度瑞兹方法联合求解多个宏观状态下的细胞问题。

Result: 所提方法具有高精度、高效率，并能获得宏观状态与梯度上的连续可微细胞响应表示；与传统FE²方法相比，大幅加速了宏观尺度问题的求解过程。

Conclusion: 参数化深度瑞兹方法为非线性均质化提供了高效、可微的细胞问题求解框架，适用于大规模多尺度模拟，具备良好的工程应用前景。

Abstract: Elliptic homogenization is used to determine coarse-grained properties of materials with features on small scales. When these small scale features have rapid, periodic fluctuations, the solution field corresponding to a homogenized constitutive relation closely resembles the true solution based on the heterogeneous material. This homogenized behavior of the material is computed from a cell problem, where a cell is defined to be one period of the fluctuating material. In the context of linear elliptic partial differential equations, the homogenized constitutive relation is defined simply by a constant coefficient tensor, but for nonlinear problems, the homogenized response depends on the macroscopic state and/or its gradient, thus requiring solutions to parametric cell problems. When computing a numerical solution with the homogenized constitutive relation, it is useful to have a differentiable representation of the solution to the cell problem, as derivatives of the homogenized constitutive relation are required in Newton iterations for the macroscopic state field. In this work, we use the Deep Ritz method to solve the parametric cell problems that arise from nonlinear homogenization. First, we exploit the variational structure of the cell problem, then we discretize the dependence of the cell response on both space and the macroscopic state with a neural network. Enforcing boundary conditions on the cell response strongly, we next use the parametric Deep Ritz method to simultaneously solve the cell problem over a range of macroscopic states. We show that this method is accurate, efficient, and offers a continuous and differentiable representation of the cell response over the macroscopic state and gradient. We then show that our parametric representation of the cell response significantly expedites macroscale solutions when compared to a traditional $\text{FE}^2$ scheme.

</details>


### [85] [Online Learning with LLM Experts from Limited Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05820)
*Wang Wei,Soumyabrata Pal,Koyel Mukherjee,Franck Dernoncourt,Ryan A. Rossi,Branislav Kveton,Hoda Eldardiry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在在线设置下，如何自适应地将提示路由到大型语言模型（LLM）专家以最大化响应质量，仅依赖有限反馈。将问题建模为带$K$个动作（代表专家）和$d$个特征（编码提示）的多臂赌博机问题，在$T$轮内最小化累积遗憾。提出算法在全信息设置下实现$\tilde{O}(d T / \sqrt{m})$的遗憾，在赌博机设置下实现$\tilde{O}(d T \sqrt{K / m})$，其中$m \\ll T$为反馈预算。实验表明，该方法能高效学习高质量的路由策略，适用于多种LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在在线环境中，如何基于有限反馈自适应地选择最优的LLM专家来处理提示，以提升响应质量，是关键挑战。现有方法难以在反馈受限条件下有效学习路由策略。

Method: 将提示路由问题形式化为多臂赌博机问题，利用上下文特征建模提示，设计基于上下文的自适应算法，在全信息与部分信息（带奖赏观测）场景下分别优化策略选择，通过探索-利用平衡降低遗憾。

Result: 在全信息设置下，达到$\tilde{O}(d T / \sqrt{m})$的遗憾界；在带奖赏观测的带状设置下，达到$\tilde{O}(d T \sqrt{K / m})$的遗憾界。实验验证了算法在多样化的大型语言模型上，能从少量反馈中高效学习高质量的路由策略。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应路由算法在反馈受限的在线设置中表现优异，能够有效提升大型语言模型的响应质量，具有良好的可扩展性和实用性。

Abstract: We study adaptive routing of prompts to large language model (LLM) experts to maximize response quality in an online setting with limited feedback. We formulate it as a bandit problem with $K$ actions that represent experts and $d$ features that encode prompts, over a horizon of $T$ rounds. We propose algorithms that strategically select and observe rewards to minimize regret. In the full-information setting, we achieve a regret of $\tilde{O}(d T / \sqrt{m})$, while in the bandit setting we achieve $\tilde{O}(d T \sqrt{K / m})$, where $m \ll T$ is a budget on feedback. Our experiments show that we efficiently learn high-quality routing strategies across diverse LLMs from limited feedback.

</details>


### [86] [Generalizing HVAC Control With Domain Randomized Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05822)
*Pablo Boitel,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NOMAD-RL 是一种基于强化学习的通用 HVAC 控制器，通过物理信息正则化流实现自适应域随机化，可在异构热区间迁移，无需侵入式接口。它利用递归策略在部分可观测条件下在线适应，显著提升跨建筑控制性能，在单/多区域场景中优于传统 PID 和非随机化 RL，接近调优良好的 MPC 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前高级 HVAC 控制器难以大规模部署，主要受限于对精确建筑模型的依赖或站点级调参需求。亟需一种无需建模、可跨不同建筑环境迁移的通用控制方法。

Method: 提出基于物理信息正则化流的自适应域随机化方案，用于建模热区参数的关联与多模态分布，结合递归策略实现在线适应，通过非侵入式恒温器接口调控设定温度。

Result: NOMAD-RL 在单区和多区测试中均显著优于常设定值 PID 和无域随机化的 RL，且在更具挑战性的多区场景中接近最优模型预测控制（MPC）性能，验证了其强泛化能力与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 自适应、物理引导的域随机化可有效提升 HVAC 控制器的迁移能力，为构建通用、可扩展的智能楼宇控制提供新路径。

Abstract: Deploying advanced HVAC (Heating, Ventilation and Air Conditioning) controllers at scale remains difficult because performance often depends on accurate building models or per-site retuning. We propose NOMAD-RL (Neural Online Meta-Adaptation for Dynamics), a general-purpose Reinforcement Learning (RL) controller designed to transfer across heterogeneous thermal zones through a universal, non-invasive thermostat interface. The controller acts on temperature setpoints from zone measurements and forecasts, while a recurrent policy supports online adaptation under partial observability.
  Our main contribution is an adaptive domain randomization scheme based on physics-informed normalizing flows, which models correlated and multimodal distributions of thermal-zone parameters while maintaining physical plausibility and controllability. This produces a realistic and progressively adaptive training curriculum that improves transfer across buildings. We evaluate NOMAD-RL against a constant-setpoint PID controller, RL without domain randomization, and MPC in single- and multi-zone settings. NOMAD-RL consistently outperforms the PID and non-randomized RL baselines, and approaches the performance of a well-tuned MPC, especially in the more challenging multi-zone case. These results highlight the potential of adaptive, physics-informed domain randomization for robust and transferable HVAC control.

</details>


### [87] [Scaling Optimal Classification Trees via Adaptive Feature and Sample Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05826)
*Jiancheng Tu,Wenqi Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于STreeD的联合特征与样本空间缩减框架，通过加权STreeD合并投影后的重复记录，减少样本相关计算；自适应STreeD则迭代优化候选特征集，保持最优树使用的特征，并重建加权表示，从而在保证解的最优性的同时显著提升计算效率。实验表明，该方法在多个数据集上相比标准STreeD提速最高达121.41倍，且在深度2至4时运行时间更短，更大深度下仍能生成可行解，性能优于或相当主流基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统动态规划求解最优分类树在特征和样本数量增加时计算开销过大，亟需高效方法以实现可扩展性。

Method: 提出Weighted STreeD与Adaptive STreeD两种策略：前者通过合并重复样本生成加权代表，减少样本计算量；后者迭代更新候选特征集，保留当前最优树所用特征，重构加权表示并求解简化问题。

Result: Weighted STreeD在实验中最快提速121.41倍；Adaptive STreeD在深度2-4时显著降低运行时间，且在更深结构中仍可生成可行解，在相同计算预算下预测性能接近或优于基准方法。

Conclusion: 联合特征与样本空间缩减能够有效提升基于动态规划的最优树学习方法的可扩展性，使其适用于更复杂的实际任务。

Abstract: Dynamic programming for optimal classification trees becomes computationally expensive as the numbers of features and training samples increase. We develop a joint feature- and sample-space reduction framework based on STreeD. Weighted STreeD merges duplicate records created after projection onto a fixed candidate set into weighted representatives. This reduces sample-dependent computation without changing the fixed-candidate optimization problem. Adaptive STreeD repeatedly refines a bounded candidate set, retains features used by the incumbent tree, rebuilds the weighted representation, and solves the resulting reduced problems. Each certified Weighted STreeD solution is optimal for its current candidate set, while the outer feature search remains heuristic over the full feature space. Experiments on five data sets show that Weighted STreeD achieves speedups of up to 121.41 times over standard STreeD. Adaptive STreeD reduces runtime in matched comparisons at depths 2 to 4 and continues to return feasible trees at greater depths where full-feature methods are limited by time or memory. Under the same computational budget, its predictive performance remains comparable to the evaluated optimal classification tree baselines and is higher in some comparisons. These results show how joint feature- and sample-space reduction can scale dynamic-programming-based optimal-tree learning to more demanding instances.

</details>


### [88] [SAFEGuard: Detect Optimization-Based Jailbreak Attacks Through Harmful Semantic Analysis and Fluency Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05850)
*Quoc Viet Vo,Trung Le,Damith C. Ranasinghe,Ehsan Abbasnejad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为SAFEGuard的统一检测框架，通过结合跨层分布距离与困惑度的混合流畅性度量以及基于梯度匹配的有害语义分析，有效应对优化型越狱攻击。该方法利用高流畅性提示保持恶意意图接近有害提示，而有害语义混淆提示常引入无意义标记序列这一关键观察，显著提升对多种越狱攻击的检测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法在应对广泛优化的越狱攻击时效果有限，尤其是那些生成高度流畅或语义混淆的有害提示的方法，因此需要更有效的检测机制。

Method: SAFEGuard框架结合了跨层分布距离和困惑度的混合流畅性测量，以及通过梯度匹配分析有害语义，以识别潜在的越狱攻击。

Result: SAFEGuard在多种优化型越狱攻击上均显著优于现有最先进基线，展现出对不断演化的越狱攻击的强大鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SAFEGuard通过融合流畅性与有害语义分析，提供了一种高效、可靠的越狱攻击检测方案，对保障大语言模型的安全部署具有重要意义。

Abstract: Despite the significant efforts devoted to aligning large language models (LLMs) with human values and ensuring safe deployment, recent work has revealed that LLMs remain vulnerable to adversarial jailbreak attacks that can bypass safety guardrails and elicit harmful responses. Many defense methods are proposed to detect jailbreaks but they are limited in their effectiveness to counter wide-range optimization-based jailbreak mechanisms that can yield highly fluency-optimized or harmful semantic obfuscated prompts. To tackle this challenge, we propose a unified detection framework SAFEGuard which incorporates a hybrid fluency measurement based on cross-layer distribution distance and perplexity, and the analysis of harmful semantics through gradient matching. Our method is grounded in a paramount observation: high fluency prompts maintain their malicious intention close to harmful prompts while harmful semantic obfuscated prompts often inject gibberish token sequences. Our evaluation demonstrates that SAFEGuard consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines and achieves significant improvement in accuracy across different optimization-based jailbreaks. This underscores the effectiveness of SAFEGuard against evolving jailbreak attacks.

</details>


### [89] [Selective Posterior Margin Regularization for Forward-Corrected Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05859)
*Zexing Zhang,Jichao Li,Tianyang Lei,XiongYi Lu,Yang Kewei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Selective Posterior Margin Regularization (SPMR)，通过利用反向后验中的类间差异，对噪声标签学习进行渐进式优化。SPMR在保持Forward校正目标的基础上，引入基于后验差距的加权更新机制，有效缓解网络对噪声标签的过拟合问题，在多个基准测试中显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理类别条件标签噪声时，尽管使用了前向校正和过渡模型，但有限样本下的神经网络仍可能记忆错误标签；同时，反向后验虽能提供对标注的置信度证据，但其主要类别与标注不一致时，次优类别之间往往接近，导致冲突难以有效利用。因此需要一种更精细的机制来区分并利用这些不确定性信号。

Method: SPMR首先计算反向后验分布，选择其中最可能的清洁类别，并根据前两个主导类别的分离程度动态调整边际损失。该方法将边际扩展为与过渡调整后的成对分离和后验质量相关的函数，通过沿最小范数逻辑方向更新以扩大选定的边际，从而实现更稳健的学习。

Result: 在五个已知过渡模型的基准上，SPMR相较于完整版Forward提升2.5-7.0个百分点，即使在使用Mixup和早停的情况下仍比Forward高出0.7-2.5个百分点。消融实验验证了后验空间系数、过渡调整目标和成对作用机制各自带来的增益。该方法可推广至估计过渡模型、人工标注、架构变化及更强的Forward策略。

Conclusion: SPMR利用已有反向后验信息，避免将所有后验冲突视为需纠正的标签，而是在局部最优方向上进行有选择性的梯度更新，实现了对噪声标签学习的高效且鲁棒的改进。

Abstract: Learning with class-conditional label noise often relies on a transition model from latent clean classes to observed annotations. Forward correction embeds this transition in the likelihood, yet finite-sample networks may still memorize corrupted labels. The corrected likelihood also induces a reverse posterior over the clean classes that could explain each annotation. When its leading class differs from the annotation, the model and transition matrix provide evidence against that annotation, but the leading alternatives can remain nearly tied. We introduce Selective Posterior Margin Regularization (SPMR), which preserves the Forward objective and converts this disagreement into a graded update on the clean classifier. SPMR selects the leading reverse-posterior class, scales a detached pairwise margin by the separation between the two leading posterior classes, and assigns correspondingly little influence to diffuse conflicts. The gap factorizes into transition- adjusted pairwise separation and the posterior mass carried by the leading pair. The active margin follows the locally minimum-norm logit direction that enlarges the selected pairwise margin. Across five known-transition benchmarks, SPMR improves full-length Forward by 2.5-7.0 percentage points and remains 0.7-2.5 percentage points above Forward with Mixup and early stopping. Matched interventions support distinct gains from the posterior-space coefficient, transition-adjusted target, and pairwise action. The same design transfers to estimated transitions, human annotations, architectural changes, and stronger Forward recipes. The formulation uses latent-class evidence already available inside Forward correction without promoting every posterior conflict to a corrected label.

</details>


### [90] [Beyond Arbitrary Geometry: Topology Generalization In neural PDE Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05860)
*Peiyao Chen,Zhouyuan Xu,Jianguo Nie,Jiansheng Fan,Chen Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究神经算子在任意网格上的泛化能力，发现拓扑变化（如隧道和空腔的改变）会影响PDE算子的不变子空间和衰减子空间。通过霍奇热流作为分析工具，提出TopoBox-3D模型，揭示了拓扑对霍奇分解中调和核与正谱的影响。实验表明，隐式推断拓扑的模型在37/45个拓扑外样本测试中表现显著下降，但调和维数变化并非主要惩罚因素；真正的难点在于谱特性——初始瑞利商是最稳定的误差预测因子，谱展宽为边和面链提供了额外信息。值得注意的是，尽管显式引入关联和调和坐标并未提升核身份准确率，但该方法在六种任务中仍取得最高非调和成分预测性能。结论指出：拓扑是超越几何兼容性的独立泛化维度，其影响贯穿整个霍奇谱而非仅限于调和核。此外，全局低频结构先验可能有助于组织快速衰减部分的预测，为神经算子跨拓扑泛化提供新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子虽被视作具有几何通用性，但其对拓扑变化的鲁棒性未被充分理解。拓扑变化会改变偏微分方程算子的不变子空间与衰减子空间，尤其影响霍奇分解中的调和核与谱结构。本文旨在揭示拓扑作为独立泛化维度的作用机制，并探索其对神经算子泛化性能的影响。

Method: 采用霍奇热流作为可控分析工具，构建TopoBox-3D数据集，系统控制隧道与空腔等拓扑特征，观察不同架构下模型在拓扑外样本（OOD）上的表现。对比六种典型神经算子架构，分析隐式与显式拓扑建模的效果，重点考察调和维数、谱特性（如瑞利商、谱展宽）、以及显式引入拓扑坐标（如关联坐标与调和坐标）的影响。

Result: 1. 隐式拓扑推理模型在37/45个拓扑外任务中出现显著性能下降；2. 调和维数变化并不显著增加误差，说明主要挑战不在调和核本身；3. 初始瑞利商是误差最稳定预测因子，谱展宽能有效提升边/面链的表征能力；4. 显式引入拓扑坐标未提升核身份准确性，但在非调和成分预测上整体最优；5. 拓扑影响贯穿整个霍奇谱，而非局限于调和核。

Conclusion: 拓扑是独立于几何的泛化轴，其影响覆盖整个霍奇谱。神经算子需考虑全局低频结构先验以增强跨拓扑泛化能力，为未来设计更鲁棒的拓扑敏感模型提供理论支持。

Abstract: Neural operators that accept arbitrary meshes are often treated as geometry-general, but unseen domain topology changes both the invariant and decaying subspaces of a PDE operator. We use Hodge heat flow as a controlled lens on this distinction and introduce TopoBox-3D, where tunnels and cavities vary Betti support while the exact Hodge decomposition separates the harmonic kernel from the positive spectrum. Across six architectures, models that infer topology implicitly suffer excess matched degradation in 37 of 45 model--task topology-OOD cells, yet cases that change harmonic dimension are not more strongly penalized on average. The dominant difficulty is instead spectral: the initial Rayleigh quotient is the most stable predictor of error, and spectral broadening adds information for edge and face cochains. Most strikingly, controlled probes show that explicit incidence and harmonic coordinates do not yield the best kernel-identity accuracy; nevertheless, TNO ranks first in mixed-input nonharmonic accuracy on all six tasks with nontrivial harmonic support. Together, these results establish topology as a distinct generalization axis beyond arbitrary-geometry compatibility and show that its influence extends across the Hodge spectrum rather than remaining confined to the harmonic kernel. More broadly, they suggest that global, low-frequency structural priors may help organize predictions in the faster-decaying complementary component, offering a new perspective on how neural operators may generalize across topology as well as geometry.

</details>


### [91] [Budgeted Task-Aware Acquisition of Dynamic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05862)
*Zihe Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出Scout框架，用于在预算有限的动态网络中进行任务感知的图信息获取。通过学习每个节点查询的任务价值，Scout在多个真实世界和合成数据集上表现出色，优于多种基线方法。实验表明，基于任务效用的监督比基于结构变化的监督更有效，且任务匹配的采样能显著提升下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态图中，由于资源受限，无法完全更新图信息，因此需要一种高效的方法来决定哪些过时的信息应被刷新以支持下游任务。现有方法未能充分考虑任务需求与观测成本之间的权衡，导致效率低下。

Method: 提出Scout框架，利用维护的图状态和观察历史，学习每个节点的查询任务价值，从而在预算约束下选择最具价值的节点进行更新。

Result: 在21个基准-预算设置中，Scout在19个场景下实现了最高的平均下游性能；在16个真实世界设置中，任务效用监督优于结构变化监督；在相同网络上，任务匹配的采集使链接预测AUC提升0.012-0.016，节点分类准确率提升0.064-0.09。

Conclusion: 图信息的有效性依赖于下游任务，通过直接从任务效用中学习，可以在有限观测预算下更有效地分配资源，显著提升模型性能。

Abstract: Learning on dynamic graphs is difficult when changes in the underlying network are only partially observed. Acquiring current graph information incurs observation and computational costs, making complete updates impractical under limited resources. This paper focuses on budgeted task-aware acquisition on dynamic networks, where a model needs to decide which stale graph information to refresh for a downstream task. We propose Scout, a lightweight framework that learns the task value of querying each node from the maintained graph and observation history. Our evaluation covers one synthetic and four real-world dynamic networks, two downstream tasks, nine acquisition baselines, and several query budgets. Scout achieves the highest mean downstream performance in 19 of the 21 benchmark-budget settings. Task-utility supervision also outperforms structural-change supervision in 13 of the 16 real-world settings. On the same dynamic network, task-matched acquisition improves link-prediction AUC by 0.012-0.016 and node-classification accuracy by 0.064-0.09 over task-mismatched acquisition. These results show that useful graph observations depend on the downstream task and that limited observation budgets can be allocated more effectively by learning directly from downstream utility.

</details>


### [92] [A budget-dependent crossover between coverage- and response-based training-set selection for machine-learned interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05877)
*Jia Bi,Alin-Marin Elena*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过预算解析比较了重训练的MACE模型在GAP-20 Carbon和合并修订版MD17数据集上的表现，探讨了结构覆盖与基于模型分歧的选择策略在不同数据保留比例下的效果。结果表明，在5%数据保留时，结构覆盖优于随机采样；而在20%时，基于响应引导的选择策略（即关注模型分歧）表现更优，显著降低力预测误差，且多数情况下优于全数据参考模型。研究揭示了保留数据预算在原子训练集选择中的决定性作用，并直接验证了响应引导压缩在特定条件下优于结构覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习势能训练集选择方法通常依赖于结构多样性或模型分歧，但哪种策略更优取决于保留的数据量，而单一预算下的比较不足以全面评估。因此需要系统分析不同预算下不同选择标准对模型精度的影响。

Method: 采用预算解析方法，对比在不同训练集规模（1%、5%、20%）下，基于结构覆盖和响应引导（模型分歧）的采样策略对MACE模型性能的影响；使用已标注数据池进行回溯测试，以评估模型分歧在压缩数据中的价值。

Result: 在5%数据保留时，结构覆盖的绝对误差小于随机采样；在20%时，响应引导选择的模型误差低于结构覆盖，平均力误差降低0.164–0.167 meV/Å，部分情况甚至低于全数据参考模型；所有八组训练种子区间均支持响应引导策略。

Conclusion: 保留数据预算是决定原子训练集选择策略的关键因素，响应引导压缩在较高数据预算下优于结构覆盖，为高效数据选择提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Selecting compact training sets for machine-learned interatomic potentials requires deciding whether to preserve structural diversity or target configurations on which models disagree. The better choice can depend on how much data is retained, making a comparison at one training-set size insufficient. Here we link selection criteria to prediction accuracy through a budget-resolved comparison of retrained MACE models on GAP-20 Carbon and pooled revised MD17. Structural coverage is compared with a response-guided selector that targets disagreement between a coverage-trained model and a full-data reference. This retrospective response witness tests the value of model disagreement for compressing an already labelled pool. At 5\%, coverage gives smaller absolute deviations from the full-data error than random sampling across four force endpoints in both datasets. The witness has larger deviations than coverage at 1\% and 5\%, but the ordering reverses at 20\%. At 20\%, witness-selected models also lower direct held-out force errors by 0.46--5.89\% relative to coverage, with all eight paired training-seed intervals favouring the witness. Six errors fall below the full-data reference. Mean force-error reductions are 0.164--0.167~meV~$\textÅ^{-1}$, with larger gains for tail and masked endpoints. Complementary analyses show that learned similarity preserves the coverage ranking, while selecting by frozen-model error gives higher error than embedding coverage. These findings establish retained-data budget as a deciding variable in atomistic training-set selection and provide a direct test of when response-guided compression improves on structural coverage.

</details>


### [93] [Grounded and Faithful P&ID Reasoning: Constraining Vision-Language Models with Recovered Evidence Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05880)
*Prathamesh Gadekar,Sagar Srinivas Sakhinana,Venkataramana Runkana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于图的视觉语言模型方法（Graph-Grounded Harness），通过从管道和仪表图（P&IDs）中显式恢复工艺连接、符号和标签构成的图结构，并限制模型仅通过七种只读操作查询该图来回答问题，从而提升拓扑判断的准确性。在新构建的TopoPID-VQA数据集上，该方法将Qwen3-VL-4B、Qwen3-VL-8B和Gemma-4-E4B的准确率从36.7–41.3%提升至74.3–76.0%，显著优于仅依赖图像提示的方法。尽管图恢复存在误差（在Digitize-PID数据集上连接F1为0.742），但结果表明：当图结构正确时，接地机制能有效提升性能，而感知错误仍会限制其表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型虽能流畅描述P&ID图纸，但常虚构或遗漏关键工艺连接，导致隔离或可达性判断错误，无法满足工业安全与维护决策对准确性的高要求。因此需要一种可验证、可追溯的推理机制，确保拓扑判断基于实际图形连接而非生成幻觉。

Method: 从原始P&ID图纸中提取符号、连接关系和标签，构建显式图结构；引入七种只读操作（如查找、遍历、连接查询等）作为唯一信息访问接口，强制模型只能通过查询该图来回答问题，实现对拓扑推理过程的可解释性和可验证性。

Result: 在TopoPID-VQA数据集上，所提方法使Qwen3-VL-4B、Qwen3-VL-8B和Gemma-4-E4B的精确匹配准确率分别达到74.3%–76.0%，相比纯图像提示方法（36.7%–41.3%）有显著提升；在不完美图重建条件下，性能提升仍明显，证明了图接地的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将视觉语言模型的推理过程锚定于从图纸中恢复的显式图结构，可以有效避免幻觉并提高拓扑问答的可靠性。该方法在真实工业场景中具备落地潜力，尤其适用于对安全性要求高的过程系统决策支持。

Abstract: Piping and Instrumentation Diagrams (P&IDs) are the authoritative maps of process plants: isolation, maintenance, and HAZOP decisions depend on what connects to what. Vision-language models describe these sheets fluently, yet they often invent or miss process connections---and an invented or missed link can reverse an isolation or reachability call, so a plant decision cannot trust a fluent answer that was never checked against the linework. We instead recover an explicit graph of the drawing---its symbols, the process connections between them, and the tags that name them---and then require the model to answer only by querying that graph through seven read-only operators, so a topology claim is returned only when it cites the query results that support it. On TopoPID-VQA, a new suite of 3000 topology questions over these sheets, Graph-Grounded Harness (Ours) raises exact match accuracy from 36.7--41.3% under image-only prompting to 74.3--76.0% for Qwen3-VL-4B, Qwen3-VL-8B, and Gemma-4-E4B. It does so on an imperfect substrate: on Digitize-PID dataset the recovered graph scores F1 0.742 on exact process connections, and 0.801 once symbols and tags are pooled in. The residual errors track that gap---grounding pays off where the recovered graph is right, and perception error still breaks topology questions where it is not.

</details>


### [94] [CALM: Class-wise Agreement and Label-gated Disagreement Modulation for Decentralized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05884)
*Yifan Ying,Qing Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CALM提出一种平滑的信任机制，通过在类别、样本和标签三个层面动态调整教师模型的可信度，解决非独立同分布（non-IID）数据下联邦蒸馏中教师目标不可靠的问题。该方法无需额外通信或辅助数据，在异构架构与严重数据偏移场景下显著优于传统平均与硬过滤策略，性能媲美甚至超越现有异构联邦学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习依赖参数平均，要求客户端使用相同模型架构，且在本地数据非独立同分布（non-IID）时性能下降。现有去中心化联邦蒸馏虽避免了对齐架构的要求，但在面对非IID数据时，对异常教师的处理采用硬阈值剔除，导致信任决策过于极端，无法灵活应对教师输出的可信程度差异。因此需要一种更鲁棒、可调节的信任机制。

Method: CALM引入三层次平滑信任门：1）按类别加权，根据教师模型对某一类预测的一致性赋予不同权重；2）按样本调整，基于教师输出与聚合目标之间的分歧程度缩放蒸馏强度；3）标签门，根据目标预测对真实标签的支持强度进一步调节蒸馏信号。所有机制均不增加通信开销或依赖外部数据。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、SVHN、OrganAMNIST和Google Speech Commands等多个数据集上，面对异构客户端架构与Dirichlet标签偏移的情况，CALM consistently优于均匀加权和硬过滤蒸馏方法，并达到或超过现有异构联邦学习方法的性能水平。

Conclusion: CALM通过引入多层次、连续的可信度评估机制，有效缓解了非IID环境下教师模型质量参差不齐带来的负面影响，实现了无需服务器、无共享架构、无额外通信的高效去中心化联邦学习，为异构环境下的模型协作提供了稳健的新范式。

Abstract: Conventional federated learning relies on parameter averaging, which forces clients to be doubly homogeneous: all must run an identical architecture, and accuracy degrades when local data are non-IID. Decentralized federated distillation sidesteps both: each client runs its peers' model snapshots as teachers on its own local data and distills from their soft predictions, with no server, no public data, and no shared architecture. Under severe non-IID skew, however, the trustworthiness of the aggregated teacher target is a matter of degree, yet existing pipelines make hard, all-or-nothing decisions: outlier teachers are discarded by threshold, and whatever target survives is trusted in full. We propose CALM, which replaces every hard decision with a smooth trust gate at three levels: per class, teachers are weighted by agreement with the peer consensus; per sample, distillation is scaled by the teachers' divergence from that target; and a label gate scales it by how strongly the target supports the sample's true label. None of this adds communication or auxiliary data. On CIFAR-10, SVHN, OrganAMNIST, and Google Speech Commands with heterogeneous client architectures under Dirichlet label skew, CALM consistently outperforms uniform and hard-filtered distillation and matches or exceeds competing heterogeneous-FL methods.

</details>


### [95] [One Rate Is Not Enough: Adaptive Anisotropic Learning Rates for LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05885)
*Huiyi Wang,Daijiao Liu,Lina Yao,Dong Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AnLR-LoRA 提出一种自适应各向异性学习率机制，为每个秩-1组件分配独立的有效学习率，基于训练时信号（如函数空间速度和Adam SNR）在线计算，并通过模块内归一化保持全局学习率预算。该方法在多个基准测试中优于传统LoRA，提升秩容量利用率，且对不同全局学习率鲁棒，可迁移至其他LoRA变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA变体采用统一学习率，忽视了模块内部各秩-1组件更新速率不均的问题，导致低速模块收敛到集中奇异谱，未能充分利用名义秩预算。

Method: 提出自适应各向异性学习率模型，基于函数空间速度和Adam SNR两个训练时信号，为每个秩-1组件动态计算有效学习率，无需额外可训练参数，实现轻量级优化。

Result: AnLR-LoRA在常识推理、自然语言生成和视觉指令微调等多个任务上均显著优于标准LoRA，增强秩容量利用，对不同学习率鲁棒，并能迁移到其他LoRA变体。

Conclusion: 通过引入自适应学习率机制，AnLR-LoRA有效缓解了传统LoRA中因统一学习率导致的秩容量浪费问题，提升了模型性能与稳定性，是一种高效且通用的参数高效微调方案。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) has become the standard for parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large language models. Most LoRA variants follow a uniform-LR convention, applying a single global learning rate across every rank-one component of every adapter. We show that this convention overlooks substantial within-module heterogeneity, where the rank-one components of a LoRA adapter update at highly uneven rates and low-velocity modules converge to concentrated singular spectra that underutilize the nominal rank budget. To address this, we propose an adaptive anisotropic learning-rate model that assigns each rank-one component its own effective learning rate, computed online from training-time signals and mean-normalized per module to preserve the global LR budget. AnLR-LoRA instantiates this model with two signals available during AdamW optimization, namely function-space velocity and Adam SNR, as a lightweight scheme with no extra trainable parameters. Across commonsense reasoning, natural language generation and visual instruction-tuning benchmarks, AnLR-LoRA consistently improves over LoRA while encouraging broader use of rank capacity, with gains that remain robust across a wide range of global learning rates and transfer cleanly to other LoRA variants.

</details>


### [96] [A First-Order Learning Algorithm for Online Resource Allocation with Constant Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05895)
*Menglong Li,Jiawei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于梯度上升的在线资源分配策略，可在有限时域内实现与事后最优解相比的常数级期望累积遗憾，且不依赖于时域长度，无需求解线性规划或非退化假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决未知请求类型概率下的有限时域在线资源分配问题，目标是实现与事后最优解接近的性能，同时避免复杂的优化计算和强假设。

Method: 采用每周期对当前请求类型对应的行动坐标进行一次梯度上升更新的原始一阶学习策略。

Result: 实现了相对于事后最优解的O(1)期望加性遗憾，该界与时域T无关，且不依赖于流形线性规划的非退化性假设。

Conclusion: 所提出的策略在无复杂计算和弱假设条件下，实现了近乎最优的在线资源分配性能，具有良好的理论保证和实际应用潜力。

Abstract: We study a finite-horizon online resource allocation problem with initial resource capacities proportional to the horizon. In each period, a request type is observed and one action is chosen from a finite menu. Each action earns a reward and consumes a vector of resources. The arrival types are independent and identically distributed, but their probabilities are unknown. We present a primal first-order learning policy that, in each period, performs one gradient ascent update of the action coordinates associated with the current request type. The policy achieves $O(1)$ expected additive regret relative to the hindsight optimum, with a bound independent of the horizon $T$. It does not solve any linear program, and the regret bound does not require a nondegeneracy assumption on the fluid linear program.

</details>


### [97] [A dictionary learning framework for graphs via filters and optimal transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05919)
*Jinchuan Liao,Dai Hai Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种图字典学习（GDL）框架，通过滤波拉普拉斯矩阵将每个图表示为零均值高斯分布，并利用滤波图距离（fGOT）计算图之间的结构相似性。通过最小化可微的近似fGOT（sfGOT）重建误差，实现端到端学习。该方法在无节点对应关系的情况下仍有效，并揭示了sfGOT与希尔伯特-施密特独立性准则的关联：最小化sfGOT等价于最大化图谱嵌入间的统计依赖性。实验表明，在图聚类和分类任务上性能优于现有GDL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图字典学习方法在处理无节点对应关系的图时存在局限，且缺乏对图间距离度量的可微性支持，限制了端到端优化能力。因此需要一种能捕捉全局结构、支持可微优化并适用于任意图结构的图表示与比较机制。

Method: 将每个图表示为基于其滤波拉普拉斯的零均值高斯分布；使用fGOT作为图间距离度量以敏感反映全局结构；引入可微的sfGOT作为fGOT的近似，支持反向传播；通过学习原子图并构建观测图的加权平均（巴氏中心）来实现图重构；利用谱嵌入与希尔伯特-施密特独立性准则建立sfGOT的统计解释。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的图聚类和分类任务中，所提方法取得了优于现有GDL方法的性能；同时证明了sfGOT最小化等价于最大化图谱嵌入间的统计依赖性，提供了新的理论视角。

Conclusion: 所提出的GDL框架结合了可微的图距离度量与统计依赖性解释，实现了对复杂图结构的有效建模与端到端优化，在图分析任务中展现出优越性能。

Abstract: We propose a graph dictionary learning (GDL) framework where each graph is represented as a zero-mean Gaussian distribution derived from its filtered Laplacian. Each observed graph is approximated by a barycenter over learned atom graphs, computed under the filter graph distance (fGOT), a graph comparison metric sensitive to global structural properties. The reconstruction error between the observed graph and its barycenter is measured by the surrogate fGOT (sfGOT) distance, a tractable approximation of fGOT that handles graphs without known node correspondence, and is minimized end-to-end via backpropagation. We further provide a novel interpretation of sfGOT through the lens of the Hilbert-Schmidt Independence Criterion, showing that minimizing the sfGOT distance between two graphs is equivalent to maximizing statistical dependence between the spectral embedding of their nodes. Experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate competitive performance over existing GDL methods on graph clustering and classification tasks.

</details>


### [98] [Rethinking the Evaluation of Efficiency Methods for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05933)
*Jiamu Zhang,Lingxi Zhang,Pengjun Lu,Qiyue Zhang,Yu-Neng Chuang,Zhengchen Li,Shuai Xu,Vipin Chaudhary,Hanjie Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个受控且针对多智能体系统（MAS）需求的诊断基准，用于评估主流的LLM-MAS效率优化方法。研究发现，现有方法报告的性能提升往往依赖于特定设置，可能源于结构坍塌、工具路径失效或初始系统本身已具备高鲁棒性，而非真正的效率改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在评估多智能体系统效率时，常使用特定提示和初始拓扑，导致性能提升难以归因于结构优化；且许多成功案例出现在非典型多智能体场景中，无法真实反映方法的有效性。因此需要一个统一、可控的基准来准确评估这些方法的真实效能。

Method: 构建一个共享骨干模型、智能体注册表和运行时环境的诊断基准，系统地测试不同方法在拓扑、规模、深度和工具使用等变量下的表现，确保评估条件一致。

Result: 分析表明，多数宣称的效率增益具有高度依赖性，部分源于结构退化或初始系统的偶然优势，并非稳健的结构性改进。

Conclusion: 当前多数效率优化方法在真实多智能体场景中的有效性被高估，亟需更严格、可复现的评估标准以推动真正高效的多智能体系统设计。

Abstract: Efficiency is increasingly important for Large Language Model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems (MAS), as larger models and more agents introduce substantial execution costs. Recent methods aim to make MAS cheaper by pruning agents, removing communication edges, or searching for compact structures. However, we argue that existing evaluations may overestimate their true ability to improve MAS efficiency. Reported gains are often measured under method-specific prompts and starting topologies, making them difficult to attribute to the proposed structural changes. Moreover, many reported successes appear in non-MAS-demanding settings, where a single agent or a randomly pruned system can already preserve strong performance. To study these issues, we introduce a controlled and MAS-demanding diagnostic benchmark for representative MAS efficiency methods. We evaluate methods under a shared backbone model, agent registry, and runtime, across controlled variations in topology, scale, depth, and tool use. Our analysis shows that many reported gains are setup-dependent and may arise from structural collapse, disabled tool pathways, or starting systems where random pruning already preserves accuracy, rather than robust improvements in MAS efficiency.

</details>


### [99] [Interpretable and Fair Generalized Additive Neural Networks via Multi-objective Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05946)
*Ziming Wang,Changwu Huang,Ke Tang,Yew-Soon Ong,Xin Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多目标神经基模型（MONBM）框架，通过多目标进化学习同时优化神经网络广义加性模型（NN-based GAMs）的准确性、可解释性和公平性，引入定量可解释性度量并分析三者间的复杂关系，验证了方法在多个维度上的竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注提升NN-based GAMs的准确性，而对其可解释性和公平性的系统评估与协同优化仍不足，尤其缺乏对三者之间权衡关系的深入理解。

Method: 提出基于多目标进化学习的MONBM框架，结合显式量化指标评估可解释性，设计部分重训练策略以适应深度模型架构，并通过实验揭示各可信维度间的内在关系。

Result: MONBM成功生成一组在准确性、可解释性与公平性间具有不同权衡的模型，验证了其优于现有先进方法的性能；揭示了三者之间的复杂相互作用及其背后原因。

Conclusion: 通过将多目标优化与自解释模型结合，MONBM为构建更可信的人工智能系统提供了有效路径，展示了在复杂权衡下实现多维可信性的可行性与优势。

Abstract: Interpretability and fairness are two of the most emphasized dimensions in trustworthy artificial intelligence (AI). Various explainable AI methods have been introduced to improve interpretability. This paper focuses on neural network (NN)-based generalized additive models (GAMs), a class of self-interpretable models. While most existing research has prioritized improving the accuracy of NN-based GAMs, their interpretability remains largely underexplored. To address this gap, this paper introduces explicit quantitative metrics for evaluating the interpretability of NN-based GAMs, empirically examines their effectiveness, and explores strategies for improving interpretability within these models. In addition, the simultaneous and explicit optimization of both interpretability and fairness, along with their trade-offs and the underlying reasons, remains underexplored. To address this, we propose a multi-objective neural basis model (MONBM) framework based on multi-objective evolutionary learning to consider accuracy, interpretability, and fairness simultaneously. A partial retraining strategy is further developed to facilitate the practical application of evolutionary multi-objective optimization to deep model architectures. Based on MONBM, this paper reveals the complex relationships between these dimensions and the reasons behind these intricate relationships. This analysis demonstrates how multi-objective optimization can be combined with self-interpretable models to reveal relationships among trustworthiness objectives. In addition, MONBM obtains a set of models with different trade-offs between dimensions, and the competitiveness of the approach is validated by comparing it with state-of-the-art methods.

</details>
